{} \textbf{Focus} & What's NEW & Everything \\
{} \textbf{Company Background} & Brief mention & 6-10 pages \\
{} \textbf{XLS Model} & Optional & Required \\
\bottomrule
\end{longtable}
\endgroup

\subparagraph{Resources}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{references/workflow.md}\mbox{}\par
Detailed Phase 1-5 instructions with step-by-step procedures for data collection, analysis, chart generation, and report creation.


\subparagraph{references/report-structure.md}\mbox{}\par
Complete page-by-page templates, table formats, and formatting requirements for the DOCX report.


\subparagraph{references/best-practices.md}\mbox{}\par
Examples of good/bad headlines, tips for success, common mistakes to avoid, and comprehensive quality checklist.


\subparagraph{Dependencies}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Required:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Python (matplotlib, pandas, seaborn) for chart generation
\item {} DOCX skill for report creation
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Optional:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} XLS skill for model updates (not required for earnings updates)
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.8em}\hrule\vspace{0.8em}

\subsection{Skill: earnings-preview}\label{file:skills-earnings-preview-SKILL-md}

\paragraph{Earnings Preview}\mbox{}\par

description: Build pre-earnings analysis with estimate models, scenario frameworks, and key metrics to watch. Use before a company reports quarterly earnings to prepare positioning notes, set up bull/bear scenarios, and identify what will move the stock. Triggers on ``earnings preview'', ``what to watch for [company] earnings'', ``pre-earnings'', ``earnings setup'', or ``preview Q[X] for [company]''.


\subparagraph{Workflow}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Step 1: Gather Context}\mbox{}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Identify the company and reporting quarter
\item {} Pull consensus estimates via web search (revenue, EPS, key segment metrics)
\item {} Find the earnings date and time (pre-market vs. after-hours)
\item {} Review the company's prior quarter earnings call for any guidance or commentary
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 2: Key Metrics Framework}\mbox{}\par

Build a ``what to watch'' framework specific to the company:


\textbf{Financial Metrics:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue vs. consensus (total and by segment)
\item {} EPS vs. consensus
\item {} Margins (gross, operating, net) — expanding or contracting?
\item {} Free cash flow
\item {} Forward guidance vs. consensus
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Operational Metrics} (sector-specific):

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Tech/SaaS: ARR, net retention, RPO, customer count
\item {} Retail: Same-store sales, traffic, basket size
\item {} Industrials: Backlog, book-to-bill, price vs. volume
\item {} Financials: NIM, credit quality, loan growth, fee income
\item {} Healthcare: Scripts, patient volumes, pipeline updates
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 3: Scenario Analysis}\mbox{}\par

Build 3 scenarios with stock price implications:


\begingroup
\small
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2pt}
\begin{longtable}{>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash\hspace{0pt}}p{0.184\textwidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash\hspace{0pt}}p{0.184\textwidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash\hspace{0pt}}p{0.184\textwidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash\hspace{0pt}}p{0.184\textwidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash\hspace{0pt}}p{0.184\textwidth}}
\toprule
{} \textbf{Scenario} & \textbf{Revenue} & \textbf{EPS} & \textbf{Key Driver} & \textbf{Stock Reaction} \\
\midrule
{} Bull &  &  &  &  \\
{} Base &  &  &  &  \\
{} Bear &  &  &  &  \\
\bottomrule
\end{longtable}
\endgroup

For each scenario:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} What would need to happen operationally
\item {} What management commentary would signal this
\item {} Historical context — how has the stock moved on similar prints?
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 4: Catalyst Checklist}\mbox{}\par

Identify the 3-5 things that will determine the stock's reaction:


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Metric] vs. [consensus/whisper number] — why it matters
\item {} [Guidance item] — what the buy-side expects to hear
\item {} [Narrative shift] — any strategic changes, M\&A, restructuring
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Step 5: Output}\mbox{}\par

One-page earnings preview with:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Company, quarter, earnings date
\item {} Consensus estimates table
\item {} Key metrics to watch (ranked by importance)
\item {} Bull/base/bear scenario table
\item {} Catalyst checklist
\item {} Trading setup: recent stock performance, implied move from options
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Important Notes}\mbox{}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Consensus estimates change — always note the source and date of estimates
\item {} ``Whisper numbers'' from buy-side surveys are often more relevant than published consensus
\item {} Historical earnings reactions help calibrate expectations (search for ``[company] earnings reaction history'')
\item {} Options-implied move tells you what the market expects — compare to your scenarios
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.8em}\hrule\vspace{0.8em}

\subsection{Skill: idea-generation}\label{file:skills-idea-generation-SKILL-md}

\paragraph{Idea Generation}\mbox{}\par

description: Systematic stock screening and investment idea sourcing. Combines quantitative screens, thematic research, and pattern recognition to surface new long and short ideas. Use when looking for new ideas, running screens, or conducting thematic sweeps. Triggers on ``idea generation'', ``stock screen'', ``find ideas'', ``what looks interesting'', ``screen for'', ``new ideas'', or ``pitch me something''.


\subparagraph{Workflow}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Step 1: Define Search Criteria}\mbox{}\par

Ask the user for parameters:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Direction}: Long ideas, short ideas, or both
\item {} \textbf{Market cap}: Large, mid, small, micro
\item {} \textbf{Sector}: Specific sector or cross-sector
\item {} \textbf{Style}: Value, growth, quality, special situation, event-driven
\item {} \textbf{Geography}: US, international, global
\item {} \textbf{Theme}: Any specific thematic angle (AI, reshoring, aging demographics, etc.)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 2: Quantitative Screens}\mbox{}\par

Run screens based on the style:


\textbf{Value Screen}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} P/E below sector median
\item {} EV/EBITDA below historical average
\item {} Free cash flow yield >5\%
\item {} Price/book below 1.5x
\item {} Insider buying in last 90 days
\item {} Dividend yield above market average
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Growth Screen}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue growth >15\% YoY
\item {} Earnings growth >20\% YoY
\item {} Revenue acceleration (growth rate increasing)
\item {} Expanding margins
\item {} High return on invested capital (>15\%)
\item {} Strong net retention (>110\% for SaaS)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Quality Screen}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Consistent revenue growth (5+ years)
\item {} Stable or expanding margins
\item {} ROE >15\%
\item {} Low debt/equity
\item {} High free cash flow conversion
\item {} Insider ownership >5\%
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Short Screen}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Declining revenue or decelerating growth
\item {} Margin compression
\item {} Rising receivables / inventory vs. sales
\item {} Insider selling
\item {} Valuation premium to peers without justification
\item {} High short interest with deteriorating fundamentals
\item {} Accounting red flags (auditor changes, restatements)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Special Situation Screen}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Recent IPOs / SPACs with lockup expirations
\item {} Spin-offs in last 12 months
\item {} Companies emerging from restructuring
\item {} Activist involvement
\item {} Management changes at underperforming companies
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 3: Thematic Sweep}\mbox{}\par

For thematic ideas, research the theme and identify beneficiaries:


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Define the thesis (e.g., ``AI infrastructure spending accelerates through 2026'')
\item {} Map the value chain — who benefits directly vs. indirectly?
\item {} Identify pure-play vs. diversified exposure
\item {} Assess which names are already ``priced in'' vs. under-appreciated
\item {} Look for second-order beneficiaries that the market hasn't connected to the theme
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Step 4: Idea Presentation}\mbox{}\par

For each idea that passes the screen, present:


\textbf{[Company Name] — [Long/Short] — [One-Line Thesis]}


\begingroup
\small
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2pt}
\begin{longtable}{>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash\hspace{0pt}}p{0.320\textwidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash\hspace{0pt}}p{0.320\textwidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash\hspace{0pt}}p{0.320\textwidth}}
\toprule
{} \textbf{Metric} & \textbf{Value} & \textbf{vs. Peers} \\
\midrule
{} Market cap &  &  \\
{} EV/ EBITDA (NTM) &  &  \\
{} P/ E (NTM) &  &  \\
{} Revenue growth &  &  \\
{} EBITDA margin &  &  \\
{} FCF yield &  &  \\
\bottomrule
\end{longtable}
\endgroup

\textbf{Thesis (3-5 bullets):}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Why this is mispriced
\item {} What the market is missing
\item {} Catalyst to realize value
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Key Risks:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} What would make this wrong
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Suggested Next Steps:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Build full model? Deep-dive diligence? Expert call?
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 5: Output}\mbox{}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Shortlist of 5-10 ideas with one-page summaries
\item {} Screening criteria and methodology documented
\item {} Comparison table across all ideas
\item {} Prioritized list: which ideas to research first
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Important Notes}\mbox{}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Screens surface candidates, not conclusions — every screen output needs fundamental work
\item {} The best ideas often come from intersections (e.g., quality company at value price due to temporary headwind)
\item {} Avoid crowded trades — check ownership data, short interest, and how many analysts cover the name
\item {} Contrarian ideas need a catalyst — being early without a catalyst is the same as being wrong
\item {} Track idea hit rates over time — which screens and approaches produce the best ideas?
\item {} Short ideas need higher conviction — timing is harder and risk is asymmetric
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.8em}\hrule\vspace{0.8em}

\subsection{Skill Resource: skills/initiating-coverage/assets/quality-checklist.md}\label{file:skills-initiating-coverage-assets-quality-checklist-md}

\paragraph{Quality Control Checklist for Initiation Reports}\mbox{}\par

Before delivering an initiation report, verify all items below are complete.


\subparagraph{Critical Minimums - Reports Must Meet These}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{CRITICAL DO NOT DELIVER IF:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} ❌ DOCX report fewer than 30 pages → INCOMPLETE
\item {} ❌ Fewer than 25 embedded charts → INCOMPLETE
\item {} ❌ Fewer than 12 comprehensive tables → INCOMPLETE
\item {} ❌ Fewer than 10,000 words → INCOMPLETE
\item {} ❌ No XLS financial model → MISSING DELIVERABLE
\item {} ❌ Charts are text descriptions, not actual PNG/JPG files → MAJOR FAILURE
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Deliverables Checklist}\mbox{}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox DOCX report file created
\item {} \UncheckedBox XLS financial model file created
\item {} \UncheckedBox Both files named properly: \emph{[Company]\_\allowbreak{}Initiation\_\allowbreak{}Report\_\allowbreak{}[Date].docx} and \emph{[Company]\_\allowbreak{}Financial\_\allowbreak{}Model\_\allowbreak{}[Date].xlsx}
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{DOCX Report - Length \& Content}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Length Verification:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Report is 30-50 pages (count pages in final document)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Word count is 10,000-15,000 words
\item {} \UncheckedBox If under 30 pages: STOP and add more content
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Visual Elements:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox 25-35 charts embedded (count them: \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} charts)
\item {} \UncheckedBox All charts are actual PNG/JPG image files (NOT text descriptions)
\item {} \UncheckedBox 12-20 comprehensive tables included (count them: \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} tables)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Charts and tables interspersed throughout, not grouped at end
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Chart Requirements:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue by product chart: Stacked Area format ✓
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue by geography chart: Stacked Bar format ✓
\item {} \UncheckedBox DCF sensitivity: 2-way Heat Map with color coding ✓
\item {} \UncheckedBox Valuation football field: Horizontal bar chart ✓
\item {} \UncheckedBox All other charts are actual image files ✓
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Table Requirements:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Full Income Statement (40-50 rows) with 5 years historical + 5 years projected
\item {} \UncheckedBox Full Cash Flow Statement (30-40 rows)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Full Balance Sheet (35-45 rows)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue by product table (20-30 rows)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue by geography table (15-20 rows)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue by channel table (10-15 rows)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Comparable companies table with statistical summary (max/75th/median/25th/min)
\item {} \UncheckedBox DCF calculation table (30-40 rows)
\item {} \UncheckedBox WACC calculation table (8-10 rows)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Two sensitivity tables
\item {} \UncheckedBox 2-3 additional financial/competitive tables
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{DOCX Report - Structure}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Page 1 Requirements:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox ``INITIATING COVERAGE'' header present (NOT ``Company Update'')
\item {} \UncheckedBox Thesis-focused title (NOT event-driven like ``Strong Q4 Results'')
\item {} \UncheckedBox Rating box with rating, price, target price, 52-week range, market cap, EV
\item {} \UncheckedBox 3-4 paragraph-length bullets with ■ character and bold headers
\item {} \UncheckedBox Financial \& valuation metrics table with 2-3 years historical, 2 years projected
\item {} \UncheckedBox Table shows ``A'' suffix for actuals, ``E'' suffix for estimates
\item {} \UncheckedBox Source lines on all visuals
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Content Sections:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Table of Contents (Page 2)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Investment Thesis \& Risks (3-5 pages)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Company Overview (6-12 pages) including:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Company description
\item {} \UncheckedBox History and milestones
\item {} \UncheckedBox Management bios (300-400 words EACH for 3-4 executives)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Products/services detail
\item {} \UncheckedBox Competitive landscape
\end{itemize}
\item {} \UncheckedBox Financial Analysis \& Projections (10-15 pages)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Valuation Analysis (8-12 pages)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Assumptions section (2,000-3,000 words documenting ALL projection assumptions)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Scenario Analysis (1,500-2,000 words with Bull/Base/Bear parameters)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Appendices including Data Sources \& References page
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{DOCX Report - Formatting}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Figure \& Table Formatting:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every figure has caption above: ``Figure X - [Company] [Descriptive Title]''
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every figure has source line below: ``Source: [Specific sources with dates]''
\item {} \UncheckedBox Sequential figure numbering (Figure 1, 2, 3... no gaps)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every table has header row with shading
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every table has source line at bottom
\item {} \UncheckedBox All years use ``A'' for actual, ``E'' for estimate notation
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Professional Formatting:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Consistent fonts throughout (Calibri, Arial, or similar)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Headers and footers with page numbers
\item {} \UncheckedBox Dense layout: 60-80\% page coverage, minimal white space
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every page has both text AND visuals (charts or tables)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Professional business report template used
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Citations \& Sources ⭐⭐⭐ CRITICAL}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Source Attribution:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every figure has specific source with document name and date
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every table has specific source with document reference
\item {} \UncheckedBox Key statistics throughout text have footnotes with sources
\item {} \UncheckedBox NOT just generic ``Company data'' - must be specific
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Hyperlinks:} ⭐⭐⭐ MANDATORY

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox ALL URLs are CLICKABLE HYPERLINKS (not plain text)
\item {} \UncheckedBox SEC filings hyperlinked to EDGAR viewer
\item {} \UncheckedBox Earnings transcripts hyperlinked (Seeking Alpha or company IR)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Press releases hyperlinked to company IR page
\item {} \UncheckedBox Presentations hyperlinked to PDF URLs
\item {} \UncheckedBox Industry reports hyperlinked (if publicly available)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Subscription data (Bloomberg, FactSet) noted as ``(subscription required)''
\item {} \UncheckedBox No raw URLs displayed anywhere - all formatted as hyperlinks
\item {} \UncheckedBox Test 3-5 sample hyperlinks to ensure they work (Ctrl+Click)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Reference Page:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox ``Data Sources \& References'' page at end of report
\item {} \UncheckedBox Lists ALL sources used in report
\item {} \UncheckedBox Sources organized by category (SEC Filings, Earnings Transcripts, etc.)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every source has date
\item {} \UncheckedBox Every source has clickable hyperlink (where applicable)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{XLS Financial Model - Structure}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{File Structure:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox 15+ tabs in Excel workbook
\item {} \UncheckedBox Tabs include: Executive Summary, Assumptions, Historical Financials, Revenue Model, Operating Expenses, Income Statement, Balance Sheet, Cash Flow, Supporting Schedules, DCF Valuation, Comps Analysis, Precedent Transactions, Scenarios, Sensitivity Analysis, Charts
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Formatting:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Blue text for hardcoded inputs
\item {} \UncheckedBox Black text for formulas
\item {} \UncheckedBox Green text for links to other sheets
\item {} \UncheckedBox Professional formatting with borders and shading
\item {} \UncheckedBox Clear section headers and labels
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Model Functionality:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox All numbers flow (change assumption → entire model updates)
\item {} \UncheckedBox DCF links to assumptions and projections
\item {} \UncheckedBox No circular references or errors
\item {} \UncheckedBox All important cells/ranges are named
\item {} \UncheckedBox Sensitivity tables work dynamically
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{XLS Financial Model - Content}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Projections:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox 3-5 years historical data
\item {} \UncheckedBox 5 years forward projections (FY+1 through FY+5)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue broken down by product, geography, channel
\item {} \UncheckedBox Full P\&L with 40-50 line items
\item {} \UncheckedBox Full cash flow with 30-40 line items
\item {} \UncheckedBox Full balance sheet with 35-45 line items
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Valuation:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Complete DCF model with all calculations shown
\item {} \UncheckedBox WACC calculation with all components
\item {} \UncheckedBox Terminal value calculation
\item {} \UncheckedBox Comparable companies analysis (5-10 companies)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Precedent transactions analysis (5-10 deals)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Scenario analysis (Bull/Base/Bear)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Two sensitivity tables
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Cross-File Consistency}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{CRITICAL}: Numbers must match EXACTLY between DOCX and XLS


\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue numbers match across both files
\item {} \UncheckedBox EPS numbers match across both files
\item {} \UncheckedBox Margin percentages match across both files
\item {} \UncheckedBox Valuation numbers match across both files
\item {} \UncheckedBox Price target matches across both files
\item {} \UncheckedBox All projected years match across both files
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Verification Method}: Spot check 10-15 key numbers between DOCX report and XLS model.


\subparagraph{Content Quality}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Investment Thesis:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox 3-5 clear thesis pillars
\item {} \UncheckedBox Each pillar supported with specific data and quantification
\item {} \UncheckedBox Financial impact quantified for each pillar
\item {} \UncheckedBox Catalysts identified with timelines
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Analysis Depth:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Comprehensive business model analysis
\item {} \UncheckedBox Detailed competitive assessment
\item {} \UncheckedBox 3-5 year financial trends analyzed
\item {} \UncheckedBox 8-12 risks identified and quantified
\item {} \UncheckedBox Management team analyzed (300-400 words per executive)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Assumptions:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox 2,000-3,000 words documenting ALL assumptions
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue growth assumptions by category/geography
\item {} \UncheckedBox Margin assumptions with bridge showing drivers
\item {} \UncheckedBox Working capital assumptions
\item {} \UncheckedBox CapEx assumptions
\item {} \UncheckedBox Each assumption has specific quantification
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Scenarios:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox 1,500-2,000 words on scenario analysis
\item {} \UncheckedBox Bull case with specific parameters and catalysts
\item {} \UncheckedBox Base case with detailed rationale
\item {} \UncheckedBox Bear case with specific triggers
\item {} \UncheckedBox Probability assessments for each scenario
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Writing Quality}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Style:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Lead with numbers (``Revenue grew 15\% to \$1.2B'' not ``Strong revenue'')
\item {} \UncheckedBox Use ``vs.'' not ``versus''
\item {} \UncheckedBox Be direct and concise
\item {} \UncheckedBox Professional institutional tone throughout
\item {} \UncheckedBox No informal language
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Accuracy:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox No typos in ticker symbol
\item {} \UncheckedBox No typos in company name
\item {} \UncheckedBox All dates accurate
\item {} \UncheckedBox All calculations verified
\item {} \UncheckedBox Charts match text descriptions
\item {} \UncheckedBox All numbers properly formatted (\$ signs, \% signs, commas)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Pre-Delivery Final Check}\mbox{}\par

Run through this quick final review:


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Deliverables}: Both DOCX and XLS files created ✓
\item {} \textbf{Length}: DOCX is 30-50 pages ✓
\item {} \textbf{Charts}: 25-35 actual PNG/JPG files embedded ✓
\item {} \textbf{Tables}: 12-20 comprehensive tables included ✓
\item {} \textbf{Words}: 10,000-15,000 words ✓
\item {} \textbf{Hyperlinks}: Test 3-5 hyperlinks - all work ✓
\item {} \textbf{Cross-check}: Spot check 10 numbers match between DOCX and XLS ✓
\item {} \textbf{Page 1}: ``INITIATING COVERAGE'' header present ✓
\end{enumerate}

If ANY item fails, DO NOT DELIVER. Go back and fix.


\subparagraph{Actual Count Verification}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Before delivery, fill in actual counts:}


DOCX Report:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Page count: \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} pages (MUST BE 30-50)
\item {} Chart count: \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} charts (MUST BE 25-35)
\item {} Table count: \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} tables (MUST BE 12-20)
\item {} Word count: \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} words (MUST BE 10,000-15,000)
\end{itemize}

XLS Model:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Tab count: \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} tabs (SHOULD BE 15+)
\item {} Model years: \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} historical + \_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{} projected
\end{itemize}

If any count is below minimum, STOP and add content before delivery.


\vspace{0.8em}\hrule\vspace{0.8em}

\subsection{Skill Resource: skills/initiating-coverage/assets/report-template.md}\label{file:skills-initiating-coverage-assets-report-template-md}

\paragraph{Equity Research Initiation Report Template}\mbox{}\par

This template provides the structure for creating a comprehensive equity research initiation report. Use this as a guide when constructing the final report document.


\textbf{NOTE:} The actual report MUST be created using the DOCX skill. DO NOT generate markdown content.


\textbf{CRITICAL REQUIREMENTS:}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Generate 20-30+ chart images} using Python (matplotlib/plotly) BEFORE creating the Word document
\item {} \textbf{Use DOCX skill}: Create professional report with proper styles, headers/footers, and formatting
\item {} \textbf{Embed actual chart images}: Insert the generated PNG/JPG chart files into the Word document at appropriate locations
\item {} \textbf{NO MARKDOWN}: Do not generate markdown content. Use DOCX skill to create a .docx file.
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{CRITICAL FORMATTING GUIDANCE:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{MAXIMUM DENSITY}: Every page should be packed with information. Intersperse text, charts, and tables throughout.
\item {} \textbf{NO ORPHANED SECTIONS}: Never have a section header alone or a single chart on its own page.
\item {} \textbf{20-30+ ACTUAL CHART IMAGES}: Generate charts as image files, then embed within text sections using DOCX skill.
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGE 1: INVESTMENT UPDATE (MOST IMPORTANT PAGE)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{CRITICAL}: Page 1 is NOT a traditional executive summary. It is an \textbf{Investment Update} with a specific institutional format used by professional equity research firms.


\textbf{IMPORTANT STRUCTURAL NOTES:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} This is an ``Investment Update'' or ``Company Update'' page, not ``Executive Summary''
\item {} Uses a rating box in top left corner
\item {} Features stock price performance chart (Figure 1) prominently
\item {} Contains 3-4 detailed bullet points with ■ character
\item {} Each bullet has \textbf{bold topic header} followed by 3-5 sentence explanation
\item {} Bottom section has financial and valuation metrics table
\item {} All charts must have figure numbers (Figure 1, Figure 2, etc.) with source lines
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Layout Structure}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{TOP LEFT - RATING BOX:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Rating: [OUTPERFORM / NEUTRAL / UNDERWEIGHT / etc.]
\item {} Price ([Date]): \$[XX.XX]
\item {} Target Price: \$[XX.XX]
\item {} 52-Week Range: \$[XX.XX] - \$[XX.XX]
\item {} Market Cap: \$[XX.X]B
\item {} Enterprise Value: \$[XX.X]B
\end{itemize}

\textbf{TOP LEFT - RESEARCH ANALYSTS:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Name], [Credentials (Ph.D., CFA, M.D., etc.)]
\item {} [Email] | [Phone]
\item {} [Name 2], [Credentials]
\item {} [Email] | [Phone]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{TOP RIGHT - STOCK PRICE PERFORMANCE:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Figure 1 - [Company Name] Stock Price Performance
\item {} [Line chart showing stock price over 12-24 months with benchmark comparison]
\item {} Source: Company data, [Firm Name] estimates.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{MAIN CONTENT - GRAY HEADER BAR:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [OUTPERFORM / NEUTRAL / etc.] RECOMMENDATION / COMPANY UPDATE
\end{itemize}

\textbf{MAIN CONTENT - DETAILED BULLETS (3-4 bullets):}


Use ■ character for bullets. Each bullet follows this format:

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} ■ \textbf{[Bold Topic Header capturing main point].} Regular text explanation providing 3-5 sentences of detail with specific numbers, comparisons, and analysis. Lead with numbers and quantification where possible. Use ``vs.'' not ``versus''. Be specific and concrete.
\item {} ■ \textbf{[Second Topic Header].} [3-5 sentences of detailed explanation...]
\item {} ■ \textbf{[Third Topic Header].} [3-5 sentences of detailed explanation...]
\item {} ■ \textbf{[Fourth Topic Header - Optional].} [3-5 sentences of detailed explanation...]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{EXAMPLE BULLET FORMAT:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} ■ \textbf{Vertical SaaS leadership and regulatory moat should enable \$50bn+ TAM by 2030.}
\item {} Deep domain expertise in healthcare IT, strong customer retention (95\%+ net revenue retention),
\item {} and cross-sell capabilities have driven Acme Health's market expansion. With the healthcare IT
\item {} market expected to reach \$50bn+ by 2030, Acme Health is well-positioned to capture share given
\item {} its regulatory moat and high switching costs. Management has indicated that 70\% of current
\item {} revenue comes from enterprise hospital systems, suggesting strong product-market fit.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{BOTTOM SECTION - FINANCIAL AND VALUATION METRICS TABLE:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Year-3]A [Year-2]A [Year-1]A [Year]E [Year+1]E
\item {} Revenue (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Revenue Growth (\%) X.X\% X.X\% X.X\% X.X\% X.X\%
\item {} Gross Margin (\%) X.X\% X.X\% X.X\% X.X\% X.X\%
\item {} EBITDA (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} EBITDA Margin (\%) X.X\% X.X\% X.X\% X.X\% X.X\%
\item {} EPS (\$) X.XX X.XX X.XX X.XX X.XX
\item {} P/E (x) XX.Xx XX.Xx XX.Xx XX.Xx XX.Xx
\item {} EV/Revenue (x) X.Xx X.Xx X.Xx X.Xx X.Xx
\item {} EV/EBITDA (x) XX.Xx XX.Xx XX.Xx XX.Xx XX.Xx
\item {} Note: Use ``A'' suffix for actual/historical years, ``E'' suffix for estimated/projected years
\item {} Source: Company data, [Firm Name] estimates.
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{FIGURE NUMBERING AND FORMATTING STANDARDS}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{CRITICAL}: All charts, graphs, and tables must follow strict figure numbering conventions used in professional equity research.


\subparagraph{Figure Numbering Format}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Every chart/table must have:}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Sequential numbering}: Figure 1, Figure 2, Figure 3, etc. (continue sequentially throughout entire report)
\item {} \textbf{Descriptive title}: ``Figure X - [Company] [Specific Metric] [Type of Chart/Analysis]''
\item {} \textbf{Source line} (always at bottom): ``Source: Company data, [Firm Name] estimates.''
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Examples:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Figure 1 - [Company] Stock Price Performance
\item {} Figure 2 - [Company] Historical and Projected Revenue Mix by Product
\item {} Figure 3 - [Company] Revenue by Geographic Region
\item {} Figure 4 - [Product Name] Revenue and Price per Patient per Year
\item {} Figure 5 - [Company] Gross Margin Evolution
\item {} Figure 6 - DCF Sensitivity Analysis (\$/share)
\item {} Figure 7 - Valuation Football Field
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Caption Format}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Figure X - [Descriptive Title]
\item {} [Chart/Table/Graph content]
\item {} Source: Company data, [Firm Name] estimates.
\end{itemize}

For tables with multiple data sources:

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Figure X - [Descriptive Title]
\item {} [Table content]
\item {} Source: Company filings, FactSet, [Firm Name] estimates.
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Placement Guidelines}\mbox{}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Figures should be numbered in order of appearance in the report
\item {} First figure (Figure 1) is typically the stock price chart or revenue growth trajectory on Page 1
\item {} Each figure must have its caption directly below the visual
\item {} Source line should be in smaller font, italicized, at the very bottom of the figure
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGE 2: TABLE OF CONTENTS}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Executive Summary....................................................1
\item {} Investment Thesis \& Risks..........................................3
\item {} Company Overview.......................................................6
\item {} Business Description \& History................................6
\item {} Management \& Ownership..........................................8
\item {} Products \& Technology...........................................9
\item {} Customers \& Go-to-Market......................................11
\item {} Growth Outlook \& Drivers...........................................13
\item {} Financial Analysis \& Performance.................................16
\item {} Historical Performance........................................16
\item {} Financial Projections.........................................19
\item {} Industry Overview \& Competitive Landscape.....................21
\item {} Market Size \& TAM..............................................21
\item {} Competitive Analysis..........................................23
\item {} Industry Trends................................................25
\item {} Valuation Analysis..................................................27
\item {} Appendices \& Disclosures...........................................31
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGES 3-5: INVESTMENT THESIS \& RISKS}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{LAYOUT PRINCIPLE}: Intersperse text with 2-3 charts in this section. Each page should have both text AND graphics. Never have pages with text only or charts only.


\subparagraph{Investment Thesis}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{[Thesis Pillar 1]: [Title - e.g., ``Large and Growing TAM'']}


[Opening sentence with key statistic]


[Paragraph 1: Market opportunity quantification]

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Current market size
\item {} Growth drivers
\item {} Company's positioning
\end{itemize}

[Paragraph 2: Why company will capture share]

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Competitive advantages
\item {} Go-to-market strategy
\item {} Early traction/proof points
\end{itemize}

[Paragraph 3: Financial impact]

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue opportunity
\item {} Margin profile
\item {} Timeline
\end{itemize}

\textbf{[EMBED CHART: TAM Growth Chart]} - Stacked area chart showing market size evolution and company's opportunity


\textbf{[Thesis Pillar 2]: [Title - e.g., ``Differentiated Technology/Product'']}


[Similar structure - 3 paragraphs covering the opportunity, competitive positioning, and financial impact]


\textbf{[EMBED CHART: Competitive Positioning Matrix]} - 2×2 chart showing company vs. competitors on key dimensions


\textbf{[Thesis Pillar 3]: [Title - e.g., ``Strong Execution and Management'']}


[Similar structure]


\textbf{[Add 2-3 more pillars as needed]}


\textbf{[EMBED CHART: Margin Expansion Pathway]} - Waterfall or line chart showing path to margin improvement


\subparagraph{Investment Risks}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Company-Specific Risks}


\textbf{[Risk 1]: [Title - e.g., ``Customer Concentration'']} [Description of risk, quantification if possible, mitigating factors. 2-3 sentences.]


\textbf{[Risk 2]: [Title - e.g., ``Execution Risk on Product Roadmap'']} [Description. 2-3 sentences.]


\textbf{[Risk 3-5]: [Additional company-specific risks]} [Continue with 3-5 total company risks]


\textbf{Industry/Market Risks}


\textbf{[Risk 1]: [Title - e.g., ``Regulatory Uncertainty'']} [Description. 2-3 sentences.]


\textbf{[Risk 2]: [Title - e.g., ``Intense Competition'']} [Description. 2-3 sentences.]


\textbf{[Risk 3-4]: [Additional industry/market risks]} [Continue with 2-4 total industry risks]


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGES 8-19: COMPANY 101}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Company Description (1 page)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Overview} [3-4 paragraphs describing:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} What the company does (in plain English)
\item {} How it makes money
\item {} Who its customers are
\item {} Geographic presence
\item {} Scale/size metrics]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Business Model Diagram/Visual} [Insert visual showing how the company creates value]


\subparagraph{Company History (2-3 pages)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{The Early Days: [Founding Story Title]}


[Paragraph on founding: who, when, why, initial vision]


\textbf{Timeline of Key Milestones}


\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Year]: [Founding event, initial funding]
\item {} [Year]: [Product launch, key milestone]
\item {} [Year]: [Major partnership, funding round]
\item {} [Year]: [Geographic expansion, new product]
\item {} [Year]: [Recent achievement]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{[Major Turning Point or Pivot]} [If applicable, describe any major strategic shifts]


\textbf{[Company Name] Today: [Current State Title]} [Paragraphs describing current position, recent developments, current strategy]


\subparagraph{Management \& Ownership (2 pages)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Key Executives}


For each executive:

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Name] - [Title]
\item {} [Bio paragraph including:
\item {} - Current role and responsibilities
\item {} - Prior experience and track record
\item {} - Key accomplishments at company
\item {} - Education/credentials]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Corporate Structure \& Governance}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Entity type (C-Corp, PBC, etc.)
\item {} Board composition
\item {} Special governance features
\item {} [Include governance diagram if applicable]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Ownership Structure} [if disclosed]

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Major shareholders and ownership \%
\item {} Strategic investors
\item {} Employee ownership
\item {} Insider ownership trends
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Core Technology/Products (2-3 pages)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Technology Overview} [Description of core technology/platform]


\textbf{Product Portfolio}


For each major product:

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Product Name]
\item {} Description:
\item {} [What it does, key features]
\item {} Target Customers:
\item {} [Who uses it, use cases]
\item {} Pricing Model:
\item {} [How it's priced, typical contract values]
\item {} Competitive Positioning:
\item {} [How it compares to alternatives]
\item {} Traction:
\item {} [Customers, revenue, growth metrics]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Product Roadmap} [Future products/features in development]


\subparagraph{Customers \& Distribution (2-3 pages)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Customer Base}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Total customers: [number]
\item {} Customer segments (Enterprise, Mid-Market, SMB)
\item {} Geographic breakdown
\item {} Customer case studies/testimonials
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Go-to-Market Strategy}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Sales channels (direct, partner, etc.)
\item {} Sales cycle and CAC
\item {} Key partnerships for distribution
\item {} Marketing strategy
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Customer Economics}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} LTV/CAC ratio
\item {} Net retention rate
\item {} Churn rates
\item {} Expansion rates
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGES 20-22: GROWTH OUTLOOK}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Growth Framework Overview}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Short-term Growth Drivers (1-2 years)}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Driver 1]
\item {} [Driver 2]
\item {} [Driver 3]
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Medium-term Growth Drivers (3-5 years)}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Driver 1]
\item {} [Driver 2]
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Detailed Growth Driver Analysis}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{[Growth Driver 1]: [Title]}


\emph{Current State:} [Baseline metrics, current performance]


\emph{Opportunity:} [Market size, company positioning, growth potential]


\emph{Timeline \& Milestones:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Near-term (1-2 years): [Expected progress]
\item {} Medium-term (3-5 years): [Expected progress]
\end{itemize}

\emph{Risks \& Challenges:} [What could prevent realization of this opportunity]


\textbf{[Repeat for each major growth driver]}


\subparagraph{Financial Projections}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Revenue Build-up} [Visual showing how revenue grows from current to projected]


\textbf{Scenario Analysis} [Table or chart showing Bear/Base/Bull case projections]


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGES 21-24: FINANCIAL ANALYSIS \& PERFORMANCE}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{LAYOUT PRINCIPLE}: This section should be VERY DENSE with 5-7 charts interspersed with financial tables. Each page should have multiple elements (table + 1-2 charts).


\subparagraph{Historical Financial Analysis}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Income Statement Highlights (3-5 Year History)}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} 2021 2022 2023 2024 LTM
\item {} Revenue (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Growth \% - X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Gross Profit (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} EBITDA (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Net Income (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} FCF (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{[CHART 1: Revenue Growth Trajectory]} Line chart showing historical revenue with annotations for key milestones. Include growth \% labels on chart.


\textbf{[CHART 2: REVENUE BY PRODUCT/SEGMENT]} ⭐ CRITICAL Stacked area chart showing revenue composition by product line or business segment over time. This shows mix shift and which products are driving growth.

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Example segments:
\item {} - Product A Revenue
\item {} - Product B Revenue
\item {} - Product C Revenue
\item {} - Services Revenue
\end{itemize}

\textbf{[CHART 3: REVENUE BY GEOGRAPHY]} ⭐ CRITICAL Stacked bar chart showing revenue breakdown by geographic region over time.

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Example regions:
\item {} - North America
\item {} - Europe
\item {} - Asia-Pacific
\item {} - Rest of World
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Financial Performance Analysis}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{[CHART 4: Gross Margin Evolution]} Line chart with annotations explaining margin drivers (scale, pricing, mix, etc.)


\textbf{[CHART 5: Operating Margin Progression]} Waterfall chart showing path from gross margin to operating margin, or line chart showing EBITDA margin trend


\textbf{[CHART 6: Free Cash Flow Generation]} Bar + line combo chart: Bars = FCF, Line = FCF margin \%


\textbf{[CHART 7: Key Operating Metrics Dashboard]} Multi-panel chart showing 3-4 key metrics:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Customer count or user growth
\item {} ARPU (Average Revenue Per User) or ACV (Annual Contract Value)
\item {} Customer cohort retention or net revenue retention
\item {} LTV/CAC or magic number or other unit economic metric
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Forward Projections (3-5 Years)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Projected Financial Model}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} 2025E 2026E 2027E 2028E 2029E
\item {} Revenue (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Growth \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Gross Profit (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} EBITDA (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} FCF (\$M) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} FCF Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Key Assumptions}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue growth drivers and assumptions
\item {} Margin progression assumptions
\item {} CapEx as \% of revenue
\item {} Working capital assumptions
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Charts:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue bridge showing growth drivers
\item {} Margin waterfall showing path to profitability/margin expansion
\item {} Free cash flow trajectory
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Fundraising \& Valuation [For Private Companies]}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Fundraising History}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Round Date Amount Valuation Lead Investor(s)
\item {} Seed [Date] \$XM \$XM [Investor]
\item {} Series A [Date] \$XM \$XM [Investor]
\item {} Series B [Date] \$XM \$XM [Investor]
\item {} [etc.]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Valuation Evolution Chart} [Visual showing valuation progression over time]


\textbf{Current Valuation Metrics}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Latest valuation: \$XXbn
\item {} Implied valuation multiple: XX.Xx
\item {} Comparison to public peers
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGES 26-31: INDUSTRY OVERVIEW}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Industry Definition \& Market Size}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Industry Overview} [2-3 paragraphs on:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Industry definition and scope
\item {} Current market size
\item {} Historical growth rates
\item {} Key trends and drivers]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Market Size Chart} [Visual showing market growth from historical through projected]


\subparagraph{Competitive Landscape}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Competitive Positioning Matrix} [2x2 chart showing company vs. competitors on key dimensions]


\textbf{Competitive Comparison Table}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Metric [Company] Comp A Comp B Comp C Comp D
\item {} Revenue (\$B) [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\item {} Growth \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Market Share X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Gross Margin X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Key Differentiator [X] [X] [X] [X] [X]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Competitive Analysis Narrative} [2-3 paragraphs analyzing:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Competitive strengths and weaknesses
\item {} Market positioning
\item {} Share gains/losses
\item {} Competitive moats]
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Total Addressable Market}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{TAM Calculation}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Current TAM (2025): \$XXbn
\item {} Projected TAM (2030): \$XXbn
\item {} CAGR: XX\%
\item {} Segmentation:
\item {} - [Segment A]: \$XXbn
\item {} - [Segment B]: \$XXbn
\item {} - [Segment C]: \$XXbn
\end{itemize}

\textbf{TAM Growth Chart} [Visual showing TAM expansion over time by segment]


\textbf{Company's Market Opportunity}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Total TAM (2030): \$XXbn
\item {} Serviceable TAM: \$XXbn
\item {} Company's Realistic Share: XX\%
\item {} Implied Revenue Potential: \$XXbn
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Industry Dynamics}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Porter's Five Forces Analysis}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Threat of new entrants: [High/Medium/Low] - [Explanation]
\item {} Bargaining power of suppliers: [High/Medium/Low] - [Explanation]
\item {} Bargaining power of buyers: [High/Medium/Low] - [Explanation]
\item {} Threat of substitutes: [High/Medium/Low] - [Explanation]
\item {} Industry rivalry: [High/Medium/Low] - [Explanation]
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Key Industry Trends}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Trend 1]: [Description and impact]
\item {} [Trend 2]: [Description and impact]
\item {} [Trend 3]: [Description and impact]
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGES 32-34: VALUATION ANALYSIS}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Valuation Methodology Summary}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Valuation Method Weight Implied Value Weighted Value
\item {} DCF Analysis 50\% \$XX - \$YY \$ZZ
\item {} Trading Comparables 30\% \$XX - \$YY \$ZZ
\item {} Precedent Transactions 20\% \$XX - \$YY \$ZZ
\item {} Weighted Average Price Target \$AA - \$BB \$CC
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{DCF Analysis}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Key Assumptions}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue Growth (2025-2029): XX\% CAGR
\item {} Terminal Growth Rate: X.X\%
\item {} WACC: X.X\%
\item {} Terminal Year EBITDA Margin: XX\%
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Figure X - DCF Sensitivity Analysis (\$/share)}


CRITICAL FORMAT: DCF sensitivity must be shown as a 2-way heat map table with color coding.


\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Terminal Growth Rate
\item {} WACC 2.0\% 2.5\% 3.0\% 3.5\% 4.0\%
\item {} 8.0\% \$52 \$55 \$58 \$62 \$66
\item {} 9.0\% \$48 \$51 \$54 \$57 \$61
\item {} 10.0\% \$45 \$47 \$50 \$53 \$56
\item {} 11.0\% \$42 \$44 \$47 \$49 \$52
\item {} 12.0\% \$39 \$41 \$44 \$46 \$49
\item {} Color coding: Green (higher values) → Yellow (mid) → Red (lower values)
\item {} Source: [Firm Name] estimates.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Scenario Analysis}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Scenario Enterprise Value Equity Value Price/Share
\item {} Bear Case \$XXbn \$XXbn \$XX
\item {} Base Case \$XXbn \$XXbn \$XX
\item {} Bull Case \$XXbn \$XXbn \$XX
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Trading Comparables}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Figure X - Comparable Companies Analysis}


CRITICAL FORMAT: Comp table must have two-part structure with statistical summary.


\textbf{Part 1: Individual Company Data}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Company Ticker Market EV/Rev EV/Rev EV/EBITDA EV/EBITDA Rev EBITDA
\item {} Cap(\$B) 2024E 2025E 2024E 2025E Growth Margin
\item {} Peer A PERA XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} Peer B PERB XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} Peer C PERC XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} Peer D PERD XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} [Company] COMP XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Part 2: Statistical Summary}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Max XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} 75th Percentile XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} Median XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} 25th Percentile XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} Min XX.X X.Xx X.Xx XX.X XX.X XX\% XX\%
\item {} Source: FactSet, company filings, [Firm Name] estimates.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Implied Valuation} [Calculation showing application of peer multiples to company's metrics]


\subparagraph{Precedent Transactions [If Applicable]}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Figure X - Precedent Transaction Analysis}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Date Target Acquirer Deal EV/Rev EV/EBITDA Premium
\item {} Value(\$B)
\item {} [MM/YYYY] [Company A] [Buyer A] X.X X.Xx XX.X XX\%
\item {} [MM/YYYY] [Company B] [Buyer B] X.X X.Xx XX.X XX\%
\item {} [MM/YYYY] [Company C] [Buyer C] X.X X.Xx XX.X XX\%
\item {} Median X.Xx XX.X XX\%
\item {} Source: Capital IQ, company filings, [Firm Name] estimates.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Control Premium Analysis} [Discussion of typical premiums in the industry]


\subparagraph{Valuation Summary}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Figure X - Valuation Football Field}


CRITICAL FORMAT: Football field must be a horizontal bar chart showing all valuation methods.


\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Valuation Method Low End Range High End
\item {} DCF Analysis \$42 ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓ \$58
\item {} Trading Comps (NTM) \$45 ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓ \$55
\item {} Precedent Trans. \$48 ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓ \$60
\item {} ↑
\item {} Current Price: \$50
\item {} Valuation Range: \$42 \$60
\item {} Color coding: Each method should have distinct color bar
\item {} Add vertical line showing current stock price
\item {} Source: [Firm Name] estimates.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Price Target \& Recommendation}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Current Price: \$XX.XX ([Date])
\item {} Price Target: \$YY.YY
\item {} Upside/Downside: ZZ\%
\item {} Recommendation: BUY / HOLD / SELL
\item {} Time Horizon: 12 months
\item {} Catalysts:
\item {} • [Near-term catalyst with timeframe]
\item {} • [Medium-term catalyst with timeframe]
\item {} • [Long-term catalyst with timeframe]
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{PAGES 35+: APPENDICES \& DISCLOSURES}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Appendix A: Detailed Financial Model}\mbox{}\par
[Reference to Excel model]


\subparagraph{Appendix B: Management Bios}\mbox{}\par
[Extended bios if not included in main text]


\subparagraph{Appendix C: Product Detail}\mbox{}\par
[Additional product information if needed]


\subparagraph{Appendix D: Industry Data Sources}\mbox{}\par
[List of sources used for industry analysis]


\subparagraph{Required Disclosures}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Analyst certification
\item {} Important disclosures
\item {} Company-specific disclosures
\item {} Legal entity disclosures
\item {} Other regulatory disclosures
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{GRAPHICS \& CHARTS TO INCLUDE}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{TARGET: 20-30+ charts throughout the report}


\textbf{CRITICAL PRINCIPLE}: Charts should be embedded within text sections, NOT grouped on separate pages. Every page (except TOC) should have at least one chart or table.


\subparagraph{Page 1 - Executive Summary (3 charts)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue/ARR growth trajectory (line chart, historical + projected)
\item {} Key metrics dashboard (multi-panel chart)
\item {} Market positioning or margin progression
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Pages 3-5 - Investment Thesis \& Risks (3 charts)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} TAM growth and opportunity (stacked area chart)
\item {} Competitive positioning matrix (2×2 with bubbles)
\item {} Margin expansion pathway (waterfall or line)
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Pages 6-17 - Company 101 (6-8 charts)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Business model diagram (flow chart)
\item {} Company timeline (horizontal timeline)
\item {} Funding history (bar chart with valuation line)
\item {} Organization chart
\item {} Product portfolio matrix
\item {} Customer segmentation (pie or tree map)
\item {} Geographic revenue breakdown
\item {} Customer cohort retention
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Pages 18-20 - Growth Outlook (4 charts)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue bridge showing drivers (waterfall)
\item {} Market share evolution (line chart)
\item {} Product roadmap (timeline)
\item {} Geographic expansion (map with timeline)
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Pages 21-24 - Financials (7 charts) ⭐ CRITICAL SECTION}\mbox{}\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue growth trajectory (line with annotations)
\item {} \textbf{Revenue by product/segment} (stacked area) ⭐ MUST HAVE
\item {} \textbf{Revenue by geography} (stacked bar) ⭐ MUST HAVE
\item {} Gross margin evolution (line chart)
\item {} Operating margin progression (waterfall or line)
\item {} Free cash flow trajectory (bar + line combo)
\item {} Key operating metrics dashboard (multi-panel)
\item {} Scenario comparison (grouped bar: Bear/Base/Bull)
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Pages 25-30 - Industry Overview (6 charts)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Market size evolution (area chart with CAGR)
\item {} Competitive landscape map (2×2)
\item {} Market share pie chart
\item {} Market share evolution over time (line chart)
\item {} TAM segmentation
\item {} Industry trend charts
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Pages 31-34 - Valuation (5 charts)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} DCF sensitivity analysis (heat map)
\item {} DCF waterfall (PV of cash flows → equity value)
\item {} Trading comps scatter plot (growth vs. multiple)
\item {} Peer valuation multiples (grouped bar)
\item {} Valuation football field (range chart)
\item {} Price target scenarios (bar with upside/downside)
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Chart Style Guidelines:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Consistent color scheme} throughout (pick 3-5 brand colors)
\item {} \textbf{Professional fonts} (Arial, Calibri, or similar)
\item {} \textbf{Clear labels and legends} on every chart
\item {} \textbf{Source citations} at bottom of each chart
\item {} \textbf{High information density} - use chart space efficiently
\item {} \textbf{Mix of chart types} for visual interest
\item {} \textbf{Annotations} to highlight key insights
\item {} \textbf{Embedded in text} - never standalone pages
\item {} \textbf{Sparklines in tables} where appropriate
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{NOTES ON USING THIS TEMPLATE}\mbox{}\par

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{PAGE 1 IS CRITICAL:} The executive summary on page 1 must contain ALL key information - fast facts, financial snapshot, 3 charts, valuation summary, thesis, and risks. This is the most important page.
\item {} \textbf{MAXIMUM DENSITY:} Professional equity research is EXTREMELY information-dense. Every page should be packed with interspersed text, charts, and tables. Aim for 60-80\% page coverage with minimal white space.
\item {} \textbf{NO ORPHANED SECTIONS:} Never have a section header alone, or a single chart/table on its own page. Always combine elements. Example: DON'T put ``Financial Snapshot'' on page 6 by itself - integrate it with surrounding content.
\item {} \textbf{20-30+ CHARTS:} Include extensive graphics throughout, with specific emphasis on:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Revenue by product/segment} (stacked area chart)
\item {} \textbf{Revenue by geography} (stacked bar chart)
\item {} \textbf{Financial performance trends} (multiple charts)
\item {} Charts should be embedded within text, not grouped separately
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Use DOC Skill:} This outline should be converted to a professional Word document using the DOC skill, with proper formatting, styles, headers/footers, and page numbers
\item {} \textbf{Intersplice Content:} Text paragraphs should have charts embedded inline. Each page should have 2-4 distinct elements (tables, charts, text blocks).
\item {} \textbf{Consistent Formatting:} Use consistent styles for headers, body text, tables, and charts throughout. Pick a color scheme and stick to it.
\item {} \textbf{References:} Include citations and sources for all data points
\item {} \textbf{Proofread:} Always proofread for accuracy, especially financial data and calculations
\item {} \textbf{Executive Summary Last:} While it appears on page 1, write this section last after completing the full analysis
\item {} \textbf{Balance:} Present both positive and negative aspects objectively
\item {} \textbf{Specific > Generic:} Use specific data and examples rather than generic statements
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.8em}\hrule\vspace{0.8em}

\subsection{Skill Resource: skills/initiating-coverage/references/task1-company-research.md}\label{file:skills-initiating-coverage-references-task1-company-research-md}

\paragraph{Task 1: Company Research - Detailed Workflow}\mbox{}\par

This document provides step-by-step instructions for executing Task 1 (Company Research) of the initiating-coverage skill.


\subparagraph{Task Overview}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Purpose}: Research company's business, management, competitive position, industry, and risks.


\textbf{Prerequisites}: ✅ None (fully independent)

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Company name or ticker symbol only
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Output}: Company Research Document (6,000-8,000 words)


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Data Sources to Gather}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Primary Sources (Company)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{SEC Filings (for public companies):}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Latest 10-K: Business description, risk factors, MD\&A, financials
\item {} Recent 10-Qs: Quarterly updates
\item {} DEF 14A (Proxy): Executive compensation, board composition
\item {} 8-Ks: Material events, acquisitions, management changes
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Company Website \& IR:}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Investor presentations
\item {} Earnings transcripts (last 2-3 quarters)
\item {} Press releases
\item {} Product documentation
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{For Private Companies:}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Company website and blog
\item {} Press releases and media coverage
\item {} LinkedIn for management bios
\item {} Crunchbase or PitchBook for funding history
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Secondary Sources (Industry/Competitive)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Competitor websites and SEC filings
\item {} Industry research reports (Gartner, Forrester, IDC, etc.)
\item {} News articles and trade publications
\item {} Market research reports
\item {} LinkedIn profiles for key executives
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Key Information to Extract}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Key Information:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Company founding date, headquarters, employee count
\item {} Revenue size and growth trajectory (if available)
\item {} Product portfolio and pricing
\item {} Customer segments and case studies
\item {} Management backgrounds and track records
\item {} Competitive landscape and market share
\item {} Industry trends and growth drivers
\item {} Regulatory considerations
\item {} High-level financial metrics (from 10-K prose, not detailed extraction)
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Step-by-Step Research Workflow}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Step 1: Initial Data Collection}\mbox{}\par

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Start with company website}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Read About/Company pages
\item {} Review product pages
\item {} Identify customer case studies
\item {} Note key metrics mentioned (employees, customers, etc.)
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Gather SEC filings (if public)}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Download latest 10-K from SEC EDGAR
\item {} Download most recent 10-Q
\item {} Download latest DEF 14A (proxy statement)
\item {} Note filing dates
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Read earnings materials}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Latest earnings transcript
\item {} Most recent investor presentation
\item {} Press releases from last 12 months
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Document basic facts}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Founding date and story
\item {} Headquarters location
\item {} Employee count
\item {} Products/services
\item {} Key customers
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Step 2: Business Model Analysis}\mbox{}\par

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Map revenue streams}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} What does the company sell?
\item {} How is it priced? (subscription, transaction, license, etc.)
\item {} Who pays?
\item {} What are typical deal sizes?
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Understand customer segments}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Enterprise vs. SMB vs. consumer
\item {} Industries served
\item {} Geographic distribution
\item {} Customer concentration (top 10 customers)
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Document go-to-market}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Direct sales vs. channel partners
\item {} Sales cycle length
\item {} Customer acquisition strategy
\item {} Distribution model
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Identify unit economics}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} LTV/CAC if available
\item {} Gross margins
\item {} Net revenue retention
\item {} Payback periods
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Step 3: Management Research}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{For each of 3-4 key executives:}


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Identify key leaders}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} CEO (always required)
\item {} CFO (always required)
\item {} COO, CTO, or other C-suite (2 additional)
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Research each executive}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Find LinkedIn profile
\item {} Review DEF 14A for background
\item {} Search for press interviews
\item {} Note tenure at company
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Write 300-400 word bio including:}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Current role and responsibilities
\item {} Prior roles and companies (last 2-3 positions)
\item {} Key accomplishments and track record
\item {} Education and credentials
\item {} Years of experience in industry
\item {} Time at current company
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Assess governance}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Board composition and independence
\item {} Key board members and their backgrounds
\item {} Insider ownership percentage
\item {} Executive compensation structure
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Step 4: Competitive Intelligence}\mbox{}\par

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Identify 5-10 competitors}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Direct competitors (same products/markets)
\item {} Indirect competitors (substitute solutions)
\item {} Emerging competitors (disruptors)
\item {} Check 10-K for company's own list of competitors
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Research each competitor}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Visit competitor website
\item {} Review their SEC filings (if public)
\item {} Note key products and positioning
\item {} Identify differentiators
\item {} Estimate market share (if data available)
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Create competitive framework}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Map on key dimensions (price, features, scale, etc.)
\item {} Identify company's competitive advantages
\item {} Note competitive vulnerabilities
\item {} Assess switching costs and network effects
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Document competitive insights}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Who are the market leaders?
\item {} Where does this company rank?
\item {} What are unique differentiators?
\item {} What are competitive threats?
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Step 5: Industry Analysis}\mbox{}\par

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Define the industry}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Industry classification (NAICS/SIC)
\item {} Scope and boundaries
\item {} Related/adjacent industries
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Size the market}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Total addressable market (TAM)
\item {} Serviceable addressable market (SAM)
\item {} Serviceable obtainable market (SOM)
\item {} Current penetration rate
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Research growth drivers}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Historical market growth rate
\item {} Projected growth rate (next 3-5 years)
\item {} Key trends accelerating/decelerating growth
\item {} Technology changes impacting industry
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Understand industry structure}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Fragmented vs. consolidated
\item {} Barriers to entry
\item {} Supplier/buyer power
\item {} Threat of substitutes
\item {} Regulatory environment
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Step 6: Risk Assessment}\mbox{}\par

Identify 8-12 risks across four categories. For each risk, write 50-100 words.


\textbf{Company-Specific Risks (4-6 risks):}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Execution risk (can management deliver?)
\item {} Customer concentration (top customers)
\item {} Key person dependency
\item {} Product/technology obsolescence
\item {} Geographic concentration
\item {} Integration risk (if recent M\&A)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Industry/Market Risks (3-4 risks):}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Competitive intensity
\item {} Regulatory changes
\item {} Technology disruption
\item {} Market saturation
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Financial Risks (2-3 risks):}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Profitability timeline
\item {} Funding requirements
\item {} Debt levels and covenants
\item {} Cash burn rate (if unprofitable)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Macroeconomic Risks (2-3 risks):}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Economic sensitivity (cyclical vs. defensive)
\item {} Interest rate sensitivity
\item {} Foreign exchange exposure
\item {} Geopolitical factors
\end{itemize}

\textbf{For each risk:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Describe the risk clearly
\item {} Quantify impact if possible
\item {} Note likelihood/severity
\item {} Identify mitigating factors
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 7: Synthesis and Writing}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Write document following this structure:}


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Company Overview} (800-1,200 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} What does the company do? (plain English)
\item {} How do they make money? (business model)
\item {} Where do they operate? (geographic presence)
\item {} How large are they? (revenue, employees, customers)
\item {} Key metrics and scale indicators
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Company History} (800-1,200 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Founding story (who, when, why, where)
\item {} Timeline of major milestones
\item {} Strategic pivots or transformations
\item {} Key acquisitions
\item {} Recent developments (last 1-2 years)
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Management Team} (1,000-1,400 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} 300-400 word bio for each of 3-4 executives
\item {} Board composition and governance
\item {} Insider ownership
\item {} Management track record assessment
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Products \& Services} (700-1,000 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Detailed product portfolio
\item {} Key features and capabilities
\item {} Product differentiation
\item {} Target customers and use cases
\item {} Pricing models and typical deal sizes
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Customers \& Go-to-Market} (500-700 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Customer segments and profiles
\item {} Distribution channels
\item {} Sales strategy and cycle
\item {} Key partnerships
\item {} Customer case studies
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Industry Overview} (800-1,200 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Industry definition and scope
\item {} Market size and structure
\item {} Growth rates (historical and projected)
\item {} Key trends and drivers
\item {} Regulatory environment
\item {} Industry dynamics
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Competitive Landscape} (700-1,000 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Analysis of 5-10 key competitors
\item {} Market positioning framework
\item {} Company's competitive advantages
\item {} Competitive vulnerabilities
\item {} Market share analysis
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Market Opportunity} (500-700 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} TAM sizing and methodology
\item {} Market growth projections
\item {} Company's serviceable market
\item {} Market share opportunity
\item {} Penetration strategy
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Risk Assessment} (600-900 words)
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Company-specific risks (4-6)
\item {} Industry/market risks (3-4)
\item {} Financial risks (2-3)
\item {} Macroeconomic risks (2-3)
\item {} Each risk: 50-100 word description
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Data Sources Section}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} List all sources used
\item {} Include dates and URLs
\item {} Organize by source type
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Quality Standards}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Content Depth}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Each section must meet minimum word count targets
\item {} Analysis should be substantive, not just descriptive
\item {} Use specific examples and quantitative data
\item {} Cite sources throughout
\item {} Maintain objectivity and balance
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Management Bios}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} 300-400 words per executive for 3-4 key executives
\item {} Must include: current role, prior experience, key accomplishments, education
\item {} Provide enough detail to assess track record and capabilities
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Competitive Analysis}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Must analyze 5-10 specific competitors
\item {} Include both direct and indirect competitors
\item {} Assess relative positioning on key dimensions
\item {} Identify company's competitive advantages and vulnerabilities
\item {} Use specific data and examples
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Risk Assessment}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Must identify 8-12 distinct risks across all four categories
\item {} Each risk needs 50-100 word description
\item {} Quantify impact where possible
\item {} Note mitigating factors
\item {} Cover all four risk categories
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Writing Quality}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Professional, analytical tone
\item {} Lead with key insights
\item {} Use concrete examples and data
\item {} Avoid generic statements
\item {} Proper citations throughout
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Output Format}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} COMPANY RESEARCH REPORT: [Company Name]
\item {} Date: [Date]
\item {} Analyst: [Your name if applicable]
\item {} TABLE OF CONTENTS
\item {} 1. Company Overview
\item {} 2. Company History
\item {} 3. Management Team
\item {} 4. Products \& Services
\item {} 5. Customers \& Go-to-Market
\item {} 6. Industry Overview
\item {} 7. Competitive Landscape
\item {} 8. Market Opportunity (TAM)
\item {} 9. Risk Assessment
\item {} 1. COMPANY OVERVIEW (800-1,200 words)
\item {} [Content]
\item {} 2. COMPANY HISTORY (800-1,200 words)
\item {} [Content]
\item {} 3. MANAGEMENT TEAM (1,000-1,400 words)
\item {} [Name], [Title]
\item {} [300-400 word bio]
\item {} [Repeat for 3-4 key executives]
\item {} [Governance section]
\item {} 4. PRODUCTS \& SERVICES (700-1,000 words)
\item {} [Content]
\item {} 5. CUSTOMERS \& GO-TO-MARKET (500-700 words)
\item {} [Content]
\item {} 6. INDUSTRY OVERVIEW (800-1,200 words)
\item {} [Content]
\item {} 7. COMPETITIVE LANDSCAPE (700-1,000 words)
\item {} [Content]
\item {} 8. MARKET OPPORTUNITY (500-700 words)
\item {} [Content]
\item {} 9. RISK ASSESSMENT (600-900 words)
\item {} Company-Specific Risks:
\item {} [4-6 risks with descriptions]
\item {} Industry/Market Risks:
\item {} [3-4 risks with descriptions]
\item {} Financial Risks:
\item {} [2-3 risks with descriptions]
\item {} Macroeconomic Risks:
