\item {} [2-3 risks with descriptions]
\item {} DATA SOURCES
\item {} [List all sources with dates and URLs]
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Success Criteria}\mbox{}\par

A successful Task 1 completion should deliver:


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Meet 6,000-8,000 word target (verify word count)
\item {} Include all 9 required sections with target word counts
\item {} Provide substantive analysis, not just description
\item {} Use specific examples and quantitative data
\item {} Cite all sources properly
\item {} Enable reader to understand:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} What the company does and how it makes money
\item {} Quality and track record of management team
\item {} Company's competitive position
\item {} Market opportunity size
\item {} Key risks to consider
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{File Naming Convention}\mbox{}\par

Save the output as:


\emph{[Company]\_\allowbreak{}Research\_\allowbreak{}Document\_\allowbreak{}[Date].md}


Example: \emph{Tesla\_\allowbreak{}Research\_\allowbreak{}Document\_\allowbreak{}2024-10-27.md}


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Next Steps}\mbox{}\par

After completing Task 1, the research document will be used:


\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} As standalone company analysis
\item {} As input for Task 2 (Financial Modeling) - provides business context for projections
\item {} As input for Task 4 (Chart Generation) - provides data for company/competitive charts
\item {} As foundation for Task 5 (Report Assembly) - Company 101 sections copied verbatim
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.8em}\hrule\vspace{0.8em}

\subsection{Skill Resource: skills/initiating-coverage/references/task2-financial-modeling.md}\label{file:skills-initiating-coverage-references-task2-financial-modeling-md}

\paragraph{Task 2: Financial Modeling - Detailed Workflow}\mbox{}\par

This document provides step-by-step instructions for executing Task 2 (Financial Modeling) of the initiating-coverage skill.


\subparagraph{Task Overview}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Purpose}: Extract historical financials and build comprehensive Excel financial model with projections and scenarios.


\textbf{Prerequisites}: ⚠ Verify before starting

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Required}: Access to company financial data
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} For public companies: Latest 10-K and recent 10-Qs from SEC EDGAR
\item {} For private companies: Financial statements or estimates from available sources
\item {} OR: Pre-extracted historical financials provided by user
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Optional}: Company research (Task 1) for business context
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Output}: Excel Financial Model (.xlsx) with 6 essential tabs:

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue Model
\item {} Income Statement
\item {} Cash Flow Statement
\item {} Balance Sheet
\item {} Scenarios
\item {} DCF Inputs
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Input Verification}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{BEFORE STARTING - CHECK:}


\textbf{Option A: Extract financials directly (most common)}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Have access to 10-K filings (public company)?
\item {} \UncheckedBox OR have access to financial statements (private company)?
\item {} \UncheckedBox Ready to create Excel file for historical extraction?
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Option B: User has pre-extracted financials}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Historical financials file provided? (.xlsx or other format)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Contains 3-5 years of income statement, cash flow, balance sheet?
\item {} \UncheckedBox Data is clean and ready to use?
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Optional Context:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Company research (Task 1) complete for business understanding?
\end{itemize}

\textbf{IF VERIFICATION FAILS}: Stop and obtain access to financial statements (10-K or equivalent) before proceeding.


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Model Structure and Formatting}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Color Coding (Industry Standard)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Blue text}: Hardcoded inputs (user can change)
\item {} \textbf{Black text}: Formulas and calculations
\item {} \textbf{Green text}: Links to other sheets
\item {} \textbf{Red text}: Errors or flags (should be resolved)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Formatting Standards}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Professional borders and shading
\item {} Clear section headers
\item {} Grouped rows for collapsibility
\item {} Named ranges for key inputs/outputs
\item {} No hardcoded numbers in formulas (except constants like 12 months)
\item {} Clear units (\$ thousands, \$ millions, etc.)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Formula Best Practices}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} All numbers should flow from assumptions
\item {} Change an assumption → entire model updates
\item {} No circular references
\item {} Use named ranges for key cells
\item {} Keep formulas simple and auditable
\item {} Add comments for complex calculations
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Step-by-Step Modeling Workflow}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Step 1: Extract Historical Financials}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{If historical financials are already extracted, skip to Step 2.}


\textbf{For Public Companies:}


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Download 10-K Filing}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Go to SEC EDGAR (https://www.sec.gov/edgar/searchedgar/companysearch.html)
\item {} Search for company name or ticker
\item {} Download latest 10-K (annual report)
\item {} Navigate to Item 8: Financial Statements and Supplementary Data
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Create Historical Financials Excel File}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} File name: \emph{[Company]\_\allowbreak{}Historical\_\allowbreak{}Financials\_\allowbreak{}[Date].xlsx}
\item {} This file will be the foundation for the model
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Extract Income Statement (3-5 years)}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Create Sheet 1: ``Historical Income Statement''
\item {} Extract ALL line items for 3-5 years:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue (total and by segment if disclosed)
\item {} Cost of revenue / COGS
\item {} Gross profit
\item {} Operating expenses (R\&D, Sales \& Marketing, G\&A broken out)
\item {} EBITDA (calculate if not disclosed: EBIT + D\&A)
\item {} EBIT / Operating income
\item {} Interest expense/income
\item {} Other income/expense
\item {} Pre-tax income
\item {} Income tax and tax rate
\item {} Net income
\item {} EPS (basic and diluted)
\item {} Shares outstanding (basic and diluted)
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Extract Cash Flow Statement (3-5 years)}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Create Sheet 2: ``Historical Cash Flow''
\item {} Extract ALL line items:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Operating activities (starting from net income)
\item {} Depreciation \& amortization
\item {} Stock-based compensation
\item {} Changes in working capital (receivables, inventory, payables)
\item {} Cash from operations
\item {} Investing activities (CapEx, acquisitions)
\item {} Financing activities (debt issuance/repayment, equity, dividends)
\item {} Net change in cash
\item {} Beginning and ending cash
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Extract Balance Sheet (3-5 years)}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Create Sheet 3: ``Historical Balance Sheet''
\item {} Extract ALL line items:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Current assets (cash, receivables, inventory, other)
\item {} Non-current assets (PP\&E, intangibles, goodwill)
\item {} Total assets
\item {} Current liabilities (payables, accrued expenses, current debt)
\item {} Non-current liabilities (long-term debt, deferred taxes)
\item {} Total liabilities
\item {} Shareholders' equity (common stock, retained earnings)
\item {} Total liabilities + equity
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Calculate Historical Metrics}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Create Sheet 4: ``Historical Metrics''
\item {} Calculate from statements:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue growth \% (YoY)
\item {} Gross margin \%
\item {} EBITDA margin \%
\item {} Operating margin \%
\item {} Net margin \%
\item {} Free cash flow (CFO - CapEx)
\item {} FCF margin \%
\item {} ROIC (approximate: NOPAT / Invested Capital)
\item {} Debt/Equity ratio
\item {} Current ratio (Current Assets / Current Liabilities)
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Document Sources and Notes}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Create Sheet 5: ``Notes''
\item {} Document:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} 10-K filing date and fiscal year end
\item {} Any one-time items or adjustments noted
\item {} Non-GAAP vs GAAP differences
\item {} Segment breakdown (if revenue split by product/geography)
\item {} Data quality notes and limitations
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{For Private Companies:}


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Gather Available Data}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Financial statements (if available)
\item {} Press releases with revenue figures
\item {} Funding announcements
\item {} Industry estimates or comparable company data
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Create Simplified Historical File}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Estimated revenue (if available)
\item {} Estimated margins (from comparables if needed)
\item {} Key ratios and metrics
\item {} Document all assumptions and sources
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Verification:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox All 3 financial statements extracted (3-5 years)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Numbers reconcile across statements (net income ties)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Key metrics calculated correctly
\item {} \UncheckedBox Excel file saved and can be opened
\item {} \UncheckedBox Data sources documented (10-K dates, page numbers)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Foundation for projection model is now complete. Proceed to Step 2.}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Capital expenditures
\item {} Working capital items
\item {} Debt and interest expense
\item {} Share count (basic and diluted)
\end{itemize}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Organize historical data for entry}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Prepare 3-5 years of actuals
\item {} Will be entered directly into Income Statement, Cash Flow Statement, and Balance Sheet tabs
\item {} Historical years in columns, projected years following
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Calculate historical trends}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue CAGR
\item {} Margin progression
\item {} OpEx leverage
\item {} Working capital patterns
\item {} CapEx as \% of revenue
\item {} These trends will inform projection assumptions
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Note}: Assumptions will be documented directly in each tab as blue text inputs, not in a separate tab.


\subparagraph{Step 2: Model Revenue}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{CRITICAL: This is the most important and detailed part of the model.}


\subparagraph{A. Revenue by Product/Category (20-30 rows)}\mbox{}\par

Create detailed table:

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} 2021A 2022A 2023A 2024A 2025E 2026E 2027E 2028E 2029E
\item {} Product Category A
\item {} Sub-product A1 XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Sub-product A2 XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Sub-product A3 XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Category A Total XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Total Rev X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} YoY Growth \% - X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Product Category B
\item {} [Similar structure]
\item {} [Continue for all product categories]
\item {} Services Revenue XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Other Revenue XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} TOTAL REVENUE XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Total Revenue Growth \% - X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Key Requirements:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Show absolute revenue (\$M) for each category
\item {} Calculate \% of total revenue for each category
\item {} Show YoY growth \% for each category
\item {} Must have granular sub-categories (not just 3-5 top-level categories)
\item {} Show mix shift over time
\item {} Link all projections to Assumptions tab
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{B. Revenue by Geography (15-20 rows)}\mbox{}\par

Create detailed table:

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} 2021A 2022A 2023A 2024A 2025E 2026E 2027E 2028E 2029E
\item {} North America
\item {} United States XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Canada XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Mexico XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} NA Total XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Total X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} YoY Growth \% - X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Europe
\item {} UK XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Germany XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} France XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Other Europe XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Europe Total XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Total X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} YoY Growth \% - X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Asia-Pacific
\item {} [Similar structure]
\item {} Rest of World
\item {} [Similar structure]
\item {} TOTAL REVENUE XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Verification:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue by product total = Revenue by geography total = Total revenue
\item {} All percentages sum to 100\%
\item {} Growth rates calculated correctly
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{C. Revenue by Channel (if applicable)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} 2021A 2022A 2023A 2024A 2025E 2026E 2027E 2028E 2029E
\item {} Direct Sales XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} E-commerce/Online XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Wholesale/Partner XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Retail Stores
\item {} Company-owned stores XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Store count XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Sales per store XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Other Channels XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} TOTAL REVENUE XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 3: Model Operating Expenses}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{A. Cost of Revenue}\mbox{}\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Break down COGS components}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Product costs (materials, manufacturing)
\item {} Shipping and logistics
\item {} Service delivery costs
\item {} Other direct costs
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Link to revenue}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Calculate COGS as \% of revenue
\item {} Model gross margin by year
\item {} Link to Assumptions tab
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{B. R\&D Expenses}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Research \& Development 2021A 2022A 2023A 2024A 2025E 2026E 2027E 2028E 2029E
\item {} R\&D Headcount XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} R\&D Comp per head XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} R\&D Personnel Costs XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} R\&D Other Costs XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Total R\&D XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Revenue X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{C. Sales \& Marketing Expenses}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Sales \& Marketing 2021A 2022A 2023A 2024A 2025E 2026E 2027E 2028E 2029E
\item {} S\&M Headcount XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} S\&M Comp per head XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} S\&M Personnel Costs XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Marketing Spend XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} S\&M Other Costs XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Total S\&M XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Revenue X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{D. General \& Administrative}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} G\&A 2021A 2022A 2023A 2024A 2025E 2026E 2027E 2028E 2029E
\item {} G\&A Headcount XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} G\&A Comp per head XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} G\&A Personnel Costs XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} G\&A Other Costs XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Total G\&A XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Revenue X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{E. Depreciation \& Amortization}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Link to CapEx schedule
\item {} Apply depreciation rates from Assumptions
\item {} Calculate annual D\&A
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 4: Build Income Statement}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Create full P\&L with 40-50 line items:}


\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} INCOME STATEMENT 2021A 2022A 2023A 2024A 2025E 2026E 2027E 2028E 2029E
\item {} REVENUE
\item {} [Link to Revenue Model tab]
\item {} Total Revenue XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} YoY Growth \% - X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} COST OF REVENUE
\item {} [Link to COGS breakdown]
\item {} Total COGS XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} GROSS PROFIT XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Gross Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} OPERATING EXPENSES
\item {} Total R\&D XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Revenue X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Total S\&M XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Revenue X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Total G\&A XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Revenue X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Depreciation \& Amort. XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Total Operating Exp. XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} \% of Revenue X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} EBITDA XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} EBITDA Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} EBIT XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} EBIT Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} Interest expense (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX)
\item {} Interest income XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Other income/(expense) XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Pre-tax income XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Income tax (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX)
\item {} Tax rate \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} NET INCOME XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Net Margin \% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\% X\%
\item {} SHARES OUTSTANDING
\item {} Basic shares (M) XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} Diluted shares (M) XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX XX
\item {} EARNINGS PER SHARE
\item {} Basic EPS \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX
\item {} Diluted EPS \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX \$X.XX
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 5: Build Cash Flow Statement}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}

\item {} Accounts Receivable (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX)
\item {} Inventory (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX) (XX)

\subparagraph{Step 6: Build Balance Sheet}\mbox{}\par

Create full balance sheet with 35-45 line items:


\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]

\textbf{Balance Check Formula:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Total Assets must equal Total Liabilities + Equity for each year
\item {} Flag any imbalances in red
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 7: Build DCF Inputs Tab}\mbox{}\par

Prepare inputs for valuation (Task 3):


\textbf{Structured Template}

Create three scenarios with different assumptions:

\textbf{Document scenario rationale:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]

\item {} Base case: [Describe most likely scenario]
\item {} Bear case: [Describe downside risks and triggers]
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 9: Quality Check}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Verify model integrity:}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Test all formulas (spot check calculations)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Change assumption → verify model updates correctly
\item {} \UncheckedBox Test scenario switching
\item {} \UncheckedBox Verify color coding (blue/black/green)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Check balance sheet balances for all years
\item {} \UncheckedBox Verify no circular references (Excel will flag)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Check for hardcoded numbers in projections
\item {} \UncheckedBox Verify all cross-sheet links work
\item {} \UncheckedBox Test that revenue totals tie across all tabs
\item {} \UncheckedBox Review formatting and presentation
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Quality Standards}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Model Integrity}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} All formulas link properly across sheets
\item {} No hardcoded numbers in projections (except in Assumptions tab)
\item {} No circular references
\item {} Balance sheet balances for all years
\item {} Scenario switching works properly
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Completeness}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} All 6 essential tabs: Revenue Model, Income Statement, Cash Flow Statement, Balance Sheet, Scenarios, DCF Inputs
\item {} 40-50 line items in Income Statement
\item {} 20-30 rows in Revenue Model (product breakdown)
\item {} 15-20 rows in Revenue Model (geography breakdown)
\item {} Full cash flow and balance sheet with all line items
\item {} Bull/Base/Bear scenarios complete
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Professional Formatting}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Consistent color coding (blue/black/green)
\item {} Clear headers and labels
\item {} Proper borders and shading
\item {} Named ranges for key cells
\item {} Grouped rows for collapsibility
\item {} Units clearly labeled (\$ thousands vs. \$ millions)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Documentation}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Assumptions documented with rationale (blue text cells with comments)
\item {} Data sources noted in cell comments or notes section within tabs
\item {} Complex calculations explained with comments
\item {} Methodology described
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{File Naming Convention}\mbox{}\par

Save the financial model as: \emph{[Company]\_\allowbreak{}Financial\_\allowbreak{}Model\_\allowbreak{}[Date].xlsx}


Example: \emph{Tesla\_\allowbreak{}Financial\_\allowbreak{}Model\_\allowbreak{}2024-10-27.xlsx}


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Success Criteria}\mbox{}\par

A successful financial model should:

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Have all 6 essential tabs (Revenue Model, Income Statement, Cash Flow Statement, Balance Sheet, Scenarios, DCF Inputs)
\item {} Be fully dynamic (change assumption → model updates)
\item {} Have no hardcoded numbers in projections
\item {} Include detailed revenue breakdowns (20-30 rows by product, 15-20 rows by geography)
\item {} Contain 40-50 line items in Income Statement
\item {} Include Bull/Base/Bear scenarios
\item {} Be professionally formatted with color coding
\item {} Balance properly (balance sheet, cash flows)
\item {} Be auditable and easy to follow
\item {} Support valuation analysis with proper FCF calculations
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Common Model Types - Special Considerations}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{High-Growth Tech/SaaS}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Focus on ARR growth and net retention
\item {} Model by product line and geography
\item {} Heavy R\&D and S\&M spend
\item {} Path to profitability timeline
\item {} Unit economics (LTV/CAC)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{E-commerce/Retail}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue by product category and channel
\item {} Store count and comp store growth (if applicable)
\item {} Inventory turns and working capital
\item {} Fulfillment costs
\item {} Customer acquisition
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Manufacturing/Industrial}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Production capacity utilization
\item {} Raw material costs and pricing
\item {} Gross margin bridge (volume/price/mix/cost)
\item {} CapEx heavy models
\item {} Working capital cycles
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Next Steps}\mbox{}\par

After completing Task 2, the financial model will be used for:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Task 3 (Valuation)}: DCF inputs, projected financials
\item {} \textbf{Task 4 (Charts)}: Data for revenue trends, margin charts, scenario comparisons
\item {} \textbf{Task 5 (Report Assembly)}: Financial data for report tables and analysis
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Skill Resource: skills/initiating-coverage/references/task3-valuation.md}\label{file:skills-initiating-coverage-references-task3-valuation-md}

\paragraph{Task 3: Valuation Analysis - Detailed Workflow}\mbox{}\par

This document provides step-by-step instructions for executing Task 3 (Valuation Analysis) of the initiating-coverage skill.


\subparagraph{Task Overview}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Purpose}: Perform comprehensive valuation using DCF, comparables, and precedent transactions.


\textbf{Prerequisites}: ⚠ Verify before starting

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Required}: Financial model from Task 2
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Projected income statements
\item {} Projected cash flows
\item {} Revenue and EBITDA forecasts
\item {} DCF inputs (unlevered FCF)
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\textbf{⚠ CRITICAL: DO NOT START THIS TASK UNLESS TASK 2 IS COMPLETE}


This task requires the financial model from Task 2. Starting without it will result in incomplete work.


\textbf{IF TASK 2 IS NOT COMPLETE}: Stop immediately and inform the user that Task 2 (Financial Modeling) must be completed first. Do not attempt to proceed or create placeholder valuations.


\textbf{Output}: Valuation Analysis (4-6 pages + Excel tabs)

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} DCF analysis with sensitivity tables
\item {} Comparable companies analysis
\item {} Precedent transactions (if applicable)
\item {} Valuation football field
\item {} Price target and recommendation
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Input Verification}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{BEFORE STARTING - CHECK:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Task 2 complete? (Financial model exists)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Model file path/location known?
\item {} \UncheckedBox Can access projected financials from model?
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Required from model:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Projected FCF (5 years)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue projections
\item {} \UncheckedBox EBITDA projections
\item {} \UncheckedBox Terminal year metrics
\item {} \UncheckedBox Balance sheet data (debt, cash, shares)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{IF VERIFICATION FAILS}: Stop and complete Task 2 (Financial Modeling) before proceeding.


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Detailed Methodology Reference}\mbox{}\par

For deep dive on valuation methodologies, formulas, and theory, see: \textbf{\href{valuation-methodologies.md}{valuation-methodologies.md}}


This workflow document focuses on execution steps. Reference the methodology file for:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} DCF theory and formulas
\item {} WACC calculation details
\item {} Terminal value methods
\item {} Comparable companies theory
\item {} Precedent transactions theory
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Step-by-Step Valuation Workflow}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Step 1: Extract Data from Financial Model}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{From Task 2's financial model, extract:}


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Projected Financials (5 years)}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue by year (2025E-2029E)
\item {} EBITDA by year
\item {} EBIT by year
\item {} Tax rate
\item {} D\&A by year
\item {} CapEx by year
\item {} Change in NWC by year
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Unlevered Free Cash Flow} ``` Extract from DCF Inputs tab in financial model: 2025E   2026E   2027E   2028E   2029E EBIT            \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX × (1 - Tax Rate) = NOPAT         \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} D\&A           \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX
\item {} CapEx         (\$XX)   (\$XX)   (\$XX)   (\$XX)   (\$XX)
\item {} Chg in NWC    (\$XX)   (\$XX)   (\$XX)   (\$XX)   (\$XX) = Unlevered FCF \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX    \$XXX ```
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Balance Sheet Data (current)}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Total debt
\item {} Cash \& equivalents
\item {} Net debt (Debt - Cash)
\item {} Diluted shares outstanding
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Scenario Data}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Bull case revenue CAGR and terminal margin
\item {} Base case revenue CAGR and terminal margin
\item {} Bear case revenue CAGR and terminal margin
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subparagraph{Step 2: Build DCF Analysis}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{A. Calculate WACC}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{1. Determine Risk-Free Rate}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Use 10-year Treasury yield (check current rate)
\item {} Example: 4.0-4.5\% as of late 2024
\end{itemize}

\textbf{2. Determine Cost of Equity (CAPM)}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Cost of Equity = Risk-Free Rate + Beta × Equity Risk Premium
\item {} Inputs:
\item {} - Risk-Free Rate: [Current 10-year Treasury, e.g., 4.2\%]
\item {} - Beta: [Company beta from Bloomberg/FactSet or peer average]
\item {} - Equity Risk Premium: 5-6\% (historical average)
\item {} Example:
\item {} Cost of Equity = 4.2\% + 1.3 × 5.5\% = 11.35\%
\end{itemize}

\textbf{3. Determine Cost of Debt}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Cost of Debt = Current borrowing rate or implied yield on bonds
\item {} For private companies:
\item {} Cost of Debt = Risk-Free Rate + Credit Spread (based on rating)
\item {} Example:
\item {} Cost of Debt (pre-tax) = 6.5\%
\item {} Cost of Debt (after-tax) = 6.5\% × (1 - 25\% tax rate) = 4.875\%
\end{itemize}

\textbf{4. Determine Capital Structure}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Use market values (not book values):
\item {} Market Value of Equity (E) = Share Price × Shares Outstanding
\item {} Market Value of Debt (D) = Total Debt (use book value if bonds not traded)
\item {} Total Value (V) = E + D
\item {} Weight of Equity = E / V
\item {} Weight of Debt = D / V
\item {} Example:
\item {} E = \$5,000M (90.9\%)
\item {} D = \$500M (9.1\%)
\item {} V = \$5,500M (100\%)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{5. Calculate WACC}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} WACC = (E/V × Cost of Equity) + (D/V × Cost of Debt × (1 - Tax Rate))
\item {} Example:
\item {} WACC = (90.9\% × 11.35\%) + (9.1\% × 6.5\% × (1 - 25\%))
\item {} WACC = 10.32\% + 0.44\% = 10.76\%
\item {} Round to: 10.8\% for base case
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{B. Calculate Terminal Value}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Method 1: Perpetuity Growth (Preferred)}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Terminal Value = FCF(2029) × (1 + g) / (WACC - g)
\item {} Where:
\item {} - FCF(2029) = Final year unlevered FCF from model
\item {} - g = Perpetual growth rate (typically 2.0-3.0\%)
\item {} - Should not exceed long-term GDP growth
\item {} - Use 2.5\% as base case
\item {} Example:
\item {} FCF(2029) = \$500M
\item {} g = 2.5\%
\item {} WACC = 10.8\%
\item {} Terminal Value = \$500M × (1.025) / (0.108 - 0.025)
\item {} Terminal Value = \$512.5M / 0.083 = \$6,175M
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Method 2: Exit Multiple (Alternative)}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Terminal Value = EBITDA(2029) × Exit Multiple
\item {} Where:
\item {} - Exit Multiple = Current peer trading median (e.g., 12-15x EBITDA)
\item {} Example:
\item {} EBITDA(2029) = \$800M
\item {} Exit Multiple = 13x
\item {} Terminal Value = \$800M × 13x = \$10,400M
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Choose one method or average both.}


\subparagraph{C. Discount Cash Flows to Present Value}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} PV of Projected FCF = Σ [FCFt / (1 + WACC)\textasciicircum{}t] for t = 1 to 5
\item {} Example:
\item {} Year FCF Discount PV of FCF
\item {} (\$M) Factor (\$M)
\item {} 2025 \$250 1/(1.108)\textasciicircum{}1 = 0.9026 \$226
\item {} 2026 \$320 1/(1.108)\textasciicircum{}2 = 0.8147 \$261
\item {} 2027 \$390 1/(1.108)\textasciicircum{}3 = 0.7353 \$287
\item {} 2028 \$450 1/(1.108)\textasciicircum{}4 = 0.6636 \$299
\item {} 2029 \$500 1/(1.108)\textasciicircum{}5 = 0.5988 \$299
\item {} Total PV: \$1,372M
\item {} PV of Terminal Value = Terminal Value / (1 + WACC)\textasciicircum{}5
\item {} PV of Terminal Value = \$6,175M / (1.108)\textasciicircum{}5 = \$6,175M × 0.5988 = \$3,697M
\item {} Enterprise Value = \$1,372M + \$3,697M = \$5,069M
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{D. Calculate Equity Value and Price Per Share}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Enterprise Value \$5,069M
\item {} - Net Debt (Debt - Cash) (\$450M)
\item {} + Non-operating Assets \$0M
\item {} - Minority Interest \$0M
\item {} - Preferred Stock \$0M
\item {} = Equity Value \$4,619M
\item {} Diluted Shares Outstanding 100M
\item {} Price Per Share = \$4,619M / 100M = \$46.19
\item {} Current Stock Price: \$42.00
\item {} Implied Upside: 10.0\%
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{E. DCF Sensitivity Analysis \textbf{CRITICAL}}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Table 1: WACC vs. Terminal Growth Rate}


Create 2-way sensitivity table:

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Price Per Share (\$) Terminal Growth Rate
\item {} WACC 1.5\% 2.0\% 2.5\% 3.0\% 3.5\%
\item {} 9.0\% \$52 \$55 \$59 \$63 \$68
\item {} 9.5\% \$48 \$51 \$54 \$58 \$62
\item {} 10.0\% \$45 \$48 \$51 \$54 \$57
\item {} 10.5\% \$42 \$45 \$47 \$50 \$53
\item {} 11.0\% \$40 \$42 \$44 \$47 \$50
\item {} 11.5\% \$38 \$40 \$42 \$44 \$47
\item {} 12.0\% \$36 \$38 \$40 \$42 \$44
\item {} Base Case: WACC = 10.8\%, g = 2.5\% → \$46
\item {} Format as heatmap: Green (high values) → Yellow → Red (low values)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Table 2: Revenue CAGR vs. Terminal EBITDA Margin}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Price Per Share (\$) Terminal EBITDA Margin (2029E)
\item {} Revenue CAGR 28\% 30\% 32\% 34\% 36\%
\item {} 15\% \$38 \$42 \$46 \$50 \$54
\item {} 20\% \$42 \$46 \$51 \$56 \$61
\item {} 25\% \$46 \$51 \$56 \$62 \$68
\item {} 30\% \$51 \$56 \$62 \$68 \$75
\item {} 35\% \$56 \$62 \$68 \$75 \$83
\item {} Base Case: Rev CAGR = 25\%, EBITDA Margin = 32\% → \$56
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 3: Comparable Companies Analysis}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{A. Select Comparable Companies}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Selection Criteria:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Same industry/sector (primary requirement)
\item {} Similar business model
\item {} Comparable size (market cap, revenue)
\item {} Similar growth profile
\item {} Similar geographies
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Identify 5-10 peer companies:}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} [Peer 1] - Direct competitor
\item {} [Peer 2] - Direct competitor
\item {} [Peer 3] - Adjacent player
\item {} [Peer 4] - Similar business model
\item {} [Peer 5] - Regional competitor
\item {} [Add 3-5 more]
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Document rationale for each peer selected.}


\subparagraph{B. Gather Peer Financial Data}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{For each comparable, gather:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Current stock price
\item {} Shares outstanding (diluted)
\item {} Market capitalization
\item {} Total debt and cash (for EV calculation)
\item {} Enterprise value
\item {} LTM (Last Twelve Months) financials:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue
\item {} EBITDA
\item {} EBIT
\item {} Net Income
\end{itemize}
\item {} NTM (Next Twelve Months) consensus estimates
\item {} Revenue growth rate
\item {} EBITDA margin
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Data sources:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} FactSet, CapitalIQ, Bloomberg (preferred)
\item {} Company 10-Ks/10-Qs for actuals
\item {} Consensus estimates from Yahoo Finance, Seeking Alpha (if pro tools unavailable)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{C. Calculate Valuation Multiples}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{For each peer, calculate:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} EV/Revenue (LTM) = Enterprise Value / LTM Revenue
\item {} EV/Revenue (NTM) = Enterprise Value / NTM Revenue (est.)
\item {} EV/EBITDA (LTM) = Enterprise Value / LTM EBITDA
\item {} EV/EBITDA (NTM) = Enterprise Value / NTM EBITDA (est.)
\item {} P/E (NTM) = Market Cap / NTM Net Income (est.)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{D. Create Comparable Companies Table (MANDATORY FORMAT)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} COMPARABLE COMPANIES ANALYSIS
\item {} Company Ticker Mkt Cap EV/Rev EV/Rev EV/EBITDA EV/EBITDA P/E Rev EBITDA
\item {} (\$B) LTM NTM LTM NTM NTM Growth Margin
\item {} Peer A PRA 45.2 3.5x 3.2x 15.2x 13.8x 25x 18\% 23\%
\item {} Peer B PRB 32.8 3.2x 2.9x 14.1x 12.5x 22x 15\% 23\%
\item {} Peer C PRC 28.5 2.8x 2.6x 12.8x 11.2x 20x 12\% 22\%
\item {} Peer D PRD 52.1 4.1x 3.7x 17.5x 15.2x 29x 22\% 23\%
\item {} Peer E PRE 38.9 3.6x 3.3x 15.8x 14.1x 25x 17\% 23\%
\item {} Peer F PRF 41.2 3.7x 3.4x 16.1x 13.9x 26x 19\% 23\%
\item {} Peer G PRG 35.5 3.3x 3.0x 14.5x 12.8x 23x 16\% 22\%
\item {} [Target] TRGT 38.0 3.4x 3.1x 14.8x 13.0x 24x 17\% 23\%
\item {} STATISTICAL SUMMARY
\item {} Maximum 52.1 4.1x 3.7x 17.5x 15.2x 29x 22\% 23\%
\item {} 75th Percentile 45.2 3.7x 3.4x 16.1x 14.1x 26x 19\% 23\%
\item {} Median 38.9 3.5x 3.2x 15.2x 13.8x 25x 17\% 23\%
\item {} 25th Percentile 32.8 3.2x 2.9x 14.1x 12.5x 22x 15\% 22\%
\item {} Minimum 28.5 2.8x 2.6x 12.8x 11.2x 20x 12\% 22\%
\item {} Note: Market data as of [Date]. LTM = Last Twelve Months. NTM = Next Twelve Months.
\item {} Source: FactSet, company filings, [Analyst] estimates.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{CRITICAL}: The statistical summary (max/75th/median/25th/min) is MANDATORY.


\subparagraph{E. Apply Multiples to Target Company}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Choose primary multiple (typically EV/EBITDA for mature companies):}


\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Target Company NTM EBITDA = \$550M (from financial model)
\item {} Apply Median Peer Multiple:
\item {} Peer Median EV/EBITDA (NTM) = 13.8x
\item {} Implied EV = \$550M × 13.8x = \$7,590M
\item {} Apply 25th Percentile (Conservative):
\item {} 25th Percentile EV/EBITDA (NTM) = 12.5x
\item {} Implied EV = \$550M × 12.5x = \$6,875M
\item {} Apply 75th Percentile (Optimistic):
\item {} 75th Percentile EV/EBITDA (NTM) = 14.1x
\item {} Implied EV = \$550M × 14.1x = \$7,755M
\item {} Valuation Range (Comps): \$6,875M - \$7,755M
\item {} Midpoint: \$7,315M
\item {} Convert to Equity Value:
\item {} Implied EV (Median) \$7,590M
\item {} - Net Debt (\$450M)
\item {} = Implied Equity Value \$7,140M
\item {} Shares Outstanding 100M
\item {} Implied Price/Share \$71.40
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Justify Premium/Discount:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Target is growing 17\% vs. peer median 17\% → In-line
\item {} Target EBITDA margin 23\% vs. peer median 23\% → In-line
\item {} Target market position → [Justify premium/discount]
\item {} \textbf{Conclusion}: Apply median multiple (no adjustment)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 4: Precedent Transactions (Optional)}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Note}: Only if M\&A is relevant for this sector/company.


\subparagraph{A. Identify Relevant Transactions}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Search for 5-10 M\&A deals:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Same industry, last 3-5 years
\item {} Similar size (0.5x to 2x target's size)
\item {} Announced and closed deals
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Example:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} PRECEDENT TRANSACTIONS ANALYSIS
\item {} Date Target Acquirer Deal EV/Rev EV/EBITDA Premium Rationale
\item {} Value(\$B) LTM LTM
\item {} Q1 2024 Comp A Strategic \$5.2B 4.2x 16.5x 35\% Consolidation
\item {} Q3 2023 Comp B PE Firm \$3.8B 3.8x 14.2x 28\% Platform
\item {} Q4 2023 Comp C Strategic \$4.5B 4.0x 15.8x 32\% Geographic
\item {} Q2 2023 Comp D Strategic \$6.1B 4.5x 17.2x 38\% Strategic fit
\item {} Q1 2023 Comp E PE Firm \$3.2B 3.5x 13.5x 25\% Carve-out
\item {} Median 4.0x 15.8x 32\%
\item {} Source: CapitalIQ, company filings, press releases.
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{B. Apply to Target Company}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Target Company LTM EBITDA = \$500M
\item {} Precedent Median EV/EBITDA (LTM) = 15.8x
\item {} Implied EV (Precedent) = \$500M × 15.8x = \$7,900M
\item {} Note: Precedent multiples typically 10-20\% higher than trading comps
\item {} due to control premium and synergies.
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 5: Valuation Reconciliation}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{A. Create Valuation Summary Table}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} VALUATION SUMMARY
\item {} Method Low Base High Weight Weighted Value
\item {} DCF Analysis \$42 \$46 \$51 50\% \$23.00
\item {} Trading Comps (NTM) \$64 \$71 \$78 40\% \$28.40
\item {} Precedent Trans. \$70 \$79 \$88 10\% \$7.90
\item {} Weighted Average Target 100\% \$59.30
\item {} Rounded Price Target: \$59.00
\item {} Current Price (as of [Date]): \$42.00
\item {} Upside to Target: 40\% (\$59.00 / \$42.00 - 1)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{B. Determine Weighting Rationale}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Typical Weighting:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} DCF: 40-60\% (higher when forecasts reliable)
\item {} Trading Comps: 25-40\% (reflects market sentiment)
\item {} Precedent Trans: 10-25\% (lower unless M\&A likely)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{For this example:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} DCF 50\%: High confidence in projections
\item {} Comps 40\%: Robust peer set
\item {} Precedent 10\%: M\&A unlikely near-term
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{C. Create Valuation Football Field Chart}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} VALUATION FOOTBALL FIELD
\item {} Method Low ◄ Range ► High
\item {} DCF Analysis \$42 ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓ \$51
\item {} Trading Comps (NTM) \$64 ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓ \$78
\item {} Precedent Trans. \$70 ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓ \$88
\item {} ↑
\item {} Current: \$42
\item {} Valuation Range \$42 \$88
\item {} Price Target: \$59 (weighted average)
\item {} Color code:
\item {} - DCF: Blue
\item {} - Trading Comps: Green
\item {} - Precedent Trans: Orange
\item {} - Vertical line at current price: Red dashed
\item {} - Vertical line at target: Black solid
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{D. Scenario-Based Valuations}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} VALUATION BY SCENARIO
\item {} Scenario Probability Revenue EBITDA DCF Comps Weighted
\item {} CAGR Margin Value Multiple Avg
\item {} Bear Case 20\% 18\% 28\% \$38 11.5x \$42
\item {} Base Case 60\% 25\% 32\% \$46 13.8x \$59
\item {} Bull Case 20\% 32\% 36\% \$58 16.0x \$82
\item {} Expected Value (probability-weighted): \$59
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 6: Final Price Target \& Recommendation}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} INVESTMENT RECOMMENDATION
\item {} Current Price: \$42.00 (as of [Date])
\item {} Price Target: \$59.00 (12-month)
\item {} Upside/(Downside): +40.5\%
\item {} Rating: BUY / OUTPERFORM
\item {} Valuation Methodology: Based on weighted average of DCF (50\%),
\item {} trading comparables (40\%), and precedent
\item {} transactions (10\%).
\item {} Time Horizon: 12 months
\item {} KEY INVESTMENT CATALYSTS
\item {} 1. New Product Launch (Q2 2025)
\item {} - Expected to drive 15-20\% revenue acceleration
\item {} - Already seeing strong pre-orders
\item {} 2. Margin Expansion (FY2025-2026)
\item {} - Operating leverage from scale
\item {} - Path to 35\% EBITDA margin (from current 28\%)
\item {} 3. Market Share Gains (Ongoing)
\item {} - Taking share from legacy competitors
\item {} - Net Promoter Score improvement
\item {} 4. International Expansion (H2 2025)
\item {} - Entry into European markets
\item {} - Potential \$200M incremental revenue opportunity
\item {} 5. Potential M\&A Target (12-18 months)
\item {} - Strategic fit for larger players
\item {} - Precedent transactions suggest 30-40\% premium
\item {} KEY RISKS TO PRICE TARGET
\item {} Downside Risks:
\item {} 1. Competitive Pressure (High probability, -15\% impact)
\item {} - New entrant launched competing product
\item {} - Could pressure pricing and market share
\item {} 2. Execution Risk (Medium probability, -10\% impact)
\item {} - New product launch delays or underperformance
\item {} - Management turnover
\item {} 3. Macro Slowdown (Medium probability, -20\% impact)
\item {} - Economic recession would impact customer spending
\item {} - Operating leverage would reverse
\item {} 4. Regulatory Risk (Low probability, -25\% impact)
\item {} - Potential new regulations in key market
\item {} - Would increase compliance costs
\item {} Upside Risks:
\item {} 1. M\&A Bid (Low probability, +35\% impact)
\item {} - Strategic acquirer pays control premium
\item {} 2. Beat-and-Raise (Medium probability, +10\% impact)
\item {} - Consistent outperformance vs. estimates
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Quality Standards}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{DCF Quality Checks}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox WACC properly calculated with documented components
\item {} \UncheckedBox Terminal value reasonable (< 70\% of total enterprise value)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Sensitivity analysis covers realistic ranges (±200-300bps for WACC, ±100bps for terminal growth)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Unlevered FCF properly calculated from EBIT
\item {} \UncheckedBox Enterprise to equity value bridge correct
\item {} \UncheckedBox Share count is diluted shares, not basic
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Comparables Quality Checks}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox 5-10 comparable companies selected
\item {} \UncheckedBox Peer selection defensible (document why each peer was chosen)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Statistical summary included (max/75th/median/25th/min) - MANDATORY
\item {} \UncheckedBox Multiple selection appropriate (EV/EBITDA for mature, EV/Revenue for high-growth)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Premium/discount justified with specific factors
\item {} \UncheckedBox Data sourced properly with dates noted
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Overall Valuation Quality Checks}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox At least 2 valuation methods used (DCF + Comps minimum)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Weighting explained and appropriate
\item {} \UncheckedBox Valuation range provided (low/base/high), not just point estimate
\item {} \UncheckedBox Scenarios analyzed (Bull/Base/Bear)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Sanity checks performed (see below)
\item {} \UncheckedBox All assumptions documented with rationale
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Sanity Checks}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Always perform these validation checks:}


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Historical Multiple Check}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Is implied multiple in line with company's historical trading range?
\item {} If not, explain why
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Peer Comparison}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Is premium/discount vs. peers justified by fundamentals?
\item {} Check: growth, margins, market position
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Implied Growth Check}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} What growth is market pricing in at current price?
\item {} Is that reasonable given company trajectory?
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Market Cap Reasonableness}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Does total market cap make sense given company size and peers?
\item {} Would company be too large/small relative to industry?
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Terminal Value Check}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Is terminal value < 60-70\% of total enterprise value?
\item {} If > 70\%, projections may not be long enough
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{WACC Reasonableness}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Is WACC 8-14\% range for typical companies?
\item {} Tech/high-growth: 10-14\%
\item {} Mature/stable: 7-10\%
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Implied Returns Check}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} What IRR from current price to target over 12 months?
\item {} Is that consistent with recommendation rating?
\end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Output Files}\mbox{}\par

Create the following deliverables:


\subparagraph{1. Valuation Analysis Document}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{File}: \emph{[Company]\_\allowbreak{}Valuation\_\allowbreak{}Analysis\_\allowbreak{}[Date].md} (written analysis)


\textbf{Contents} (4-6 pages):

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Executive summary with price target
\item {} DCF analysis (1 page) with sensitivity table
\item {} Comparable companies analysis (1 page) with statistical summary
\item {} Precedent transactions (0.5 page) if applicable
\item {} Valuation summary and football field (0.5 page)
\item {} Investment recommendation (1 page)
\item {} Key catalysts and risks (1 page)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{2. Excel Valuation Tabs}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Add to Task 2's financial model file:} \emph{[Company]\_\allowbreak{}Financial\_\allowbreak{}Model\_\allowbreak{}[Date].xlsx}


\textbf{IMPORTANT}: Do NOT create a separate Excel file. Add these tabs to the existing financial model from Task 2. This keeps all quantitative data in one place.


\textbf{Tabs to add:}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} DCF tab with full calculations
\item {} Sensitivity analysis tab
\item {} Comps tab with peer data
\item {} Precedent transactions tab (if applicable)
\item {} Valuation summary tab
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Success Criteria}\mbox{}\par

A successful valuation analysis should:

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Use at least 2 methods (DCF + Comps minimum)
\item {} Include comprehensive DCF sensitivity analysis (2-way tables)
\item {} Include statistical summary in comps (max/75th/median/25th/min)
\item {} Provide valuation range (low/base/high), not point estimate
\item {} Document all key assumptions with clear rationale
\item {} Perform sanity checks
\item {} Arrive at defensible price target
\item {} Provide clear buy/hold/sell recommendation
\item {} Identify 3-5 key catalysts
\item {} Identify 3-5 key risks
\item {} Be auditable and transparent
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Next Steps}\mbox{}\par

After completing Task 3, the valuation analysis will be used for:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Task 4 (Charts)}: Create DCF sensitivity heatmaps, valuation football field, scenario comparison charts
\item {} \textbf{Task 5 (Report Assembly)}: Integrate valuation analysis into final report
\end{itemize}

The price target and recommendation are the foundation of the final investment recommendation in the equity research report.


\vspace{0.8em}\hrule\vspace{0.8em}

\subsection{Skill Resource: skills/initiating-coverage/references/task4-chart-generation.md}\label{file:skills-initiating-coverage-references-task4-chart-generation-md}

\paragraph{Task 4: Chart Generation - Detailed Workflow}\mbox{}\par

This document provides step-by-step instructions for executing Task 4 (Chart Generation) of the initiating-coverage skill.


\subparagraph{Task Overview}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Purpose}: Generate 25-35 professional financial charts for the report.


\textbf{Prerequisites}: ⚠ Verify before starting

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{Required}: Company research from Task 1
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Company history, milestones (for timeline charts)
\item {} Management team, org structure (for org charts)
\item {} Product portfolio (for product charts)
\item {} Customer segmentation (for customer charts)
\item {} Competitive landscape (for competitive positioning charts)
\item {} TAM analysis (for market size charts)
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Required}: Financial model from Task 2
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Revenue by product/geography data
\item {} Margin trends
\item {} Scenario comparison data
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Required}: Valuation analysis from Task 3
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} DCF sensitivity table
\item {} Comparable companies data
\item {} Valuation ranges
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Required}: External market data
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Historical stock price data (Yahoo Finance, Bloomberg)
\item {} Historical valuation multiples (optional for chart 34)
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\textbf{⚠ CRITICAL: DO NOT START THIS TASK UNLESS TASKS 1, 2, AND 3 ARE COMPLETE}


This task requires outputs from all three previous tasks. Starting without them will result in incomplete charts.


\textbf{IF ANY OF TASKS 1, 2, OR 3 ARE NOT COMPLETE}: Stop immediately and inform the user which tasks need to be completed first. The specific requirements are:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Task 1: Company research document (for 9 charts)
\item {} Task 2: Financial model with all 6 tabs (for 8 charts)
\item {} Task 3: Valuation tabs added to the model (for 6 charts)
\item {} External data access (for 2 charts)
\end{itemize}

Do not attempt to create placeholder charts or skip charts due to missing data.


\textbf{Output}: 25-35 Professional Chart Files (PNG/JPG, 300 DPI)


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Input Verification}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{BEFORE STARTING - CHECK ALL PREREQUISITES:}


\subparagraph{Task 1 Verification (Company Research)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Task 1 complete? (Company research document exists)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Company history and milestones documented? (for charts 05, 06)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Management team and org structure described? (for chart 07)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Product portfolio detailed? (for chart 08)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Customer segmentation analyzed? (for chart 09)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Competitive landscape mapped? (for charts 16, 17, 18)
\item {} \UncheckedBox TAM sizing completed? (for chart 15)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Task 2 Verification (Financial Model)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Task 2 complete? (Financial model Excel file exists)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue by product breakdown available? (for chart 03 ⭐)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Revenue by geography breakdown available? (for chart 04 ⭐)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Historical + projected financials complete? (for charts 02, 10, 11, 12)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Scenario analysis (Bull/Base/Bear) complete? (for chart 14)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Operating metrics available? (for chart 13)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Task 3 Verification (Valuation)}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Task 3 complete? (Valuation tabs added to model)
\item {} \UncheckedBox DCF sensitivity matrix exists? (for chart 28 ⭐)
\item {} \UncheckedBox DCF calculation details available? (for chart 29)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Comparable companies data collected? (for charts 30, 31)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Valuation ranges calculated? (for chart 32 ⭐)
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{External Data Verification}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Can access historical stock price data? (Yahoo Finance, Bloomberg for chart 01)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Can access historical valuation data? (Optional, for chart 34)
\end{itemize}

\textbf{IF ANY VERIFICATION FAILS}:

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} Missing Task 1? → Complete Task 1 (Company Research) first
\item {} Missing Task 2? → Complete Task 2 (Financial Modeling) first
\item {} Missing Task 3? → Complete Task 3 (Valuation Analysis) first
\item {} Missing external data? → Gather from Yahoo Finance, Bloomberg, or similar sources
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Chart Requirements: 25 Required + 10 Optional}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{IMPORTANT}: Task 5 (Report Assembly) will embed \textbf{ALL charts created} throughout the report. The report requires dense visual content (1 chart every 200-300 words), so create comprehensive chart coverage.


\subparagraph{4 MANDATORY Charts (Non-Negotiable) ⭐}\mbox{}\par

These 4 charts are critical visualizations that MUST be present:


\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \textbf{chart\_\allowbreak{}03}: Revenue by Product/Segment - Stacked Area Chart ⭐
\item {} \textbf{chart\_\allowbreak{}04}: Revenue by Geography - Stacked Bar Chart ⭐
\item {} \textbf{chart\_\allowbreak{}28}: DCF Sensitivity Analysis - 2-Way Heatmap ⭐
\item {} \textbf{chart\_\allowbreak{}32}: Valuation Football Field - Horizontal Bar Chart ⭐

\subparagraph{Step 3: Create Mandatory Charts}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Chart 1: Revenue by Product - Stacked Area ⭐ MANDATORY}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} def create\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}product\_\allowbreak{}chart():
\item {} ``''``Create revenue by product stacked area chart''``''
\item {} fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))
\item {} \# Create stacked area chart
\item {} ax.stackplot(years, product\_\allowbreak{}a, product\_\allowbreak{}b, product\_\allowbreak{}c, product\_\allowbreak{}d,
\item {} labels=['Product A', 'Product B', 'Product C', 'Product D'],
\item {} colors=['\#1f77b4', '\#ff7f0e', '\#2ca02c', '\#d62728'],
\item {} alpha=0.8)
\item {} \# Formatting
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}xlabel('Year', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}ylabel('Revenue (\$M)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}title('Figure 3 - Revenue by Product/Segment (2020-2029E)',
\item {} fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', pad=20)
\item {} \# Legend
\item {} ax.legend(loc='upper left', frameon=False, fontsize=10)
\item {} \# Grid
\item {} ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3, linestyle='--')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}axisbelow(True)
\item {} \# Remove top and right spines
\item {} ax.spines['top'].set\_\allowbreak{}visible(False)
\item {} ax.spines['right'].set\_\allowbreak{}visible(False)
\item {} \# Add vertical line to separate historical from projected
\item {} ax.axvline(x=2024, color='gray', linestyle='--', linewidth=1, alpha=0.5)
\item {} ax.text(2024.2, ax.get\_\allowbreak{}ylim()[1]*0.95, 'Projected →',
\item {} fontsize=9, color='gray', ha='left')
\item {} \# Source line
\item {} fig.text(0.12, 0.02, 'Source: Company data, [Firm] estimates',
\item {} fontsize=9, style='italic', color='gray')
\item {} \# Save
\item {} plt.tight\_\allowbreak{}layout()
\item {} plt.savefig('chart\_\allowbreak{}03\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}product\_\allowbreak{}stacked\_\allowbreak{}area.png',
\item {} dpi=300, bbox\_\allowbreak{}inches='tight', facecolor='white')
\item {} plt.close()
\item {} print(``✓ Created: chart\_\allowbreak{}03\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}product\_\allowbreak{}stacked\_\allowbreak{}area.png'')
\item {} create\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}product\_\allowbreak{}chart()
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Chart 2: Revenue by Geography - Stacked Bar ⭐ MANDATORY}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} def create\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}geography\_\allowbreak{}chart():
\item {} ``''``Create revenue by geography stacked bar chart''``''
\item {} years\_\allowbreak{}labels = ['2020', '2021', '2022', '2023', '2024',
\item {} '2025E', '2026E', '2027E', '2028E', '2029E']
\item {} fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))
\item {} \# Create stacked bar chart
\item {} width = 0.6
\item {} x = np.arange(len(years\_\allowbreak{}labels))
\item {} p1 = ax.bar(x, north\_\allowbreak{}america, width, label='North America', color='\#1f77b4')
\item {} p2 = ax.bar(x, europe, width, bottom=north\_\allowbreak{}america,
\item {} label='Europe', color='\#ff7f0e')
\item {} p3 = ax.bar(x, asia\_\allowbreak{}pacific, width,
\item {} bottom=np.array(north\_\allowbreak{}america) + np.array(europe),
\item {} label='Asia-Pacific', color='\#2ca02c')
\item {} p4 = ax.bar(x, rest\_\allowbreak{}of\_\allowbreak{}world, width,
\item {} bottom=np.array(north\_\allowbreak{}america) + np.array(europe) + np.array(asia\_\allowbreak{}pacific),
\item {} label='Rest of World', color='\#d62728')
\item {} \# Formatting
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}xlabel('Year', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}ylabel('Revenue (\$M)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}title('Figure 4 - Revenue by Geography (2020-2029E)',
\item {} fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', pad=20)
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}xticks(x)
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}xticklabels(years\_\allowbreak{}labels, rotation=45, ha='right')
\item {} \# Legend
\item {} ax.legend(loc='upper left', frameon=False, fontsize=10)
\item {} \# Grid
\item {} ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3, linestyle='--')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}axisbelow(True)
\item {} \# Remove top and right spines
\item {} ax.spines['top'].set\_\allowbreak{}visible(False)
\item {} ax.spines['right'].set\_\allowbreak{}visible(False)
\item {} \# Source line
\item {} fig.text(0.12, 0.02, 'Source: Company data, [Firm] estimates',
\item {} fontsize=9, style='italic', color='gray')
\item {} \# Save
\item {} plt.tight\_\allowbreak{}layout()
\item {} plt.savefig('chart\_\allowbreak{}04\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}geography\_\allowbreak{}stacked\_\allowbreak{}bar.png',
\item {} dpi=300, bbox\_\allowbreak{}inches='tight', facecolor='white')
\item {} plt.close()
\item {} print(``✓ Created: chart\_\allowbreak{}04\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}geography\_\allowbreak{}stacked\_\allowbreak{}bar.png'')
\item {} create\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}geography\_\allowbreak{}chart()
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Chart 3: DCF Sensitivity - Heatmap ⭐ MANDATORY}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} def create\_\allowbreak{}dcf\_\allowbreak{}sensitivity\_\allowbreak{}heatmap():
\item {} ``''``Create DCF sensitivity analysis heatmap''``''
\item {} \# Create DataFrame
\item {} df = pd.DataFrame(dcf\_\allowbreak{}sensitivity,
\item {} index=[f'\{w\}\%' for w in wacc\_\allowbreak{}values],
\item {} columns=[f'\{g\}\%' for g in terminal\_\allowbreak{}growth])
\item {} fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 6))
\item {} \# Create heatmap
\item {} sns.heatmap(df, annot=True, fmt='d', cmap='RdYlGn',
\item {} cbar\_\allowbreak{}kws=\{'label': 'Price per Share (\$)'\},
\item {} linewidths=0.5, linecolor='white',
\item {} ax=ax, vmin=35, vmax=95)
\item {} \# Formatting
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}xlabel('Terminal Growth Rate', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}ylabel('WACC', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}title('Figure 28 - DCF Sensitivity Analysis (\$/share)',
\item {} fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', pad=20)
\item {} \# Rotate y-axis labels
\item {} plt.yticks(rotation=0)
\item {} \# Source line
\item {} fig.text(0.12, 0.02, 'Source: [Firm] estimates',
\item {} fontsize=9, style='italic', color='gray')
\item {} \# Save
\item {} plt.tight\_\allowbreak{}layout()
\item {} plt.savefig('chart\_\allowbreak{}28\_\allowbreak{}dcf\_\allowbreak{}sensitivity\_\allowbreak{}heatmap.png',
\item {} dpi=300, bbox\_\allowbreak{}inches='tight', facecolor='white')
\item {} plt.close()
\item {} print(``✓ Created: chart\_\allowbreak{}28\_\allowbreak{}dcf\_\allowbreak{}sensitivity\_\allowbreak{}heatmap.png'')
\item {} create\_\allowbreak{}dcf\_\allowbreak{}sensitivity\_\allowbreak{}heatmap()
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Chart 4: Valuation Football Field ⭐ MANDATORY}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} def create\_\allowbreak{}valuation\_\allowbreak{}football\_\allowbreak{}field():
\item {} ``''``Create valuation football field chart''``''
\item {} fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5))
\item {} \# Create horizontal bars
\item {} y\_\allowbreak{}positions = np.arange(len(valuation\_\allowbreak{}methods))
\item {} colors = ['\#1f77b4', '\#ff7f0e', '\#2ca02c']
\item {} for i, (method, low, high, color) in enumerate(
\item {} zip(valuation\_\allowbreak{}methods, valuation\_\allowbreak{}low, valuation\_\allowbreak{}high, colors)):
\item {} ax.barh(i, high - low, left=low, height=0.6,
\item {} color=color, alpha=0.7, label=method)
\item {} \# Add value labels at ends
\item {} ax.text(low - 1, i, f'\$\{low\}', va='center', ha='right', fontsize=10)
\item {} ax.text(high + 1, i, f'\$\{high\}', va='center', ha='left', fontsize=10)
\item {} \# Add current price line
\item {} ax.axvline(x=current\_\allowbreak{}price, color='red', linestyle='--', linewidth=2,
\item {} label=f'Current: \$\{current\_\allowbreak{}price\}', alpha=0.7)
\item {} \# Add target price line
\item {} ax.axvline(x=target\_\allowbreak{}price, color='black', linestyle='-', linewidth=2,
\item {} label=f'Target: \$\{target\_\allowbreak{}price\}')
\item {} \# Formatting
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}yticks(y\_\allowbreak{}positions)
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}yticklabels(valuation\_\allowbreak{}methods, fontsize=11)
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}xlabel('Price Per Share (\$)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}title('Figure 32 - Valuation Football Field',
\item {} fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', pad=20)
\item {} \# Set x-axis limits
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}xlim(40, 70)
\item {} \# Remove spines
\item {} ax.spines['top'].set\_\allowbreak{}visible(False)
\item {} ax.spines['right'].set\_\allowbreak{}visible(False)
\item {} ax.spines['left'].set\_\allowbreak{}visible(False)
\item {} \# Grid
\item {} ax.grid(axis='x', alpha=0.3, linestyle='--')
\item {} ax.set\_\allowbreak{}axisbelow(True)
\item {} \# Legend
\item {} ax.legend(loc='upper right', frameon=False, fontsize=9)
\item {} \# Source line
\item {} fig.text(0.12, 0.02, 'Source: [Firm] estimates',
\item {} fontsize=9, style='italic', color='gray')
\item {} \# Save
\item {} plt.tight\_\allowbreak{}layout()
\item {} plt.savefig('chart\_\allowbreak{}32\_\allowbreak{}valuation\_\allowbreak{}football\_\allowbreak{}field.png',
\item {} dpi=300, bbox\_\allowbreak{}inches='tight', facecolor='white')
\item {} plt.close()
\item {} print(``✓ Created: chart\_\allowbreak{}32\_\allowbreak{}valuation\_\allowbreak{}football\_\allowbreak{}field.png'')
\item {} create\_\allowbreak{}valuation\_\allowbreak{}football\_\allowbreak{}field()
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Step 6: Quality Check}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Run verification checks:}


\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} import os
\item {} def verify\_\allowbreak{}charts():
\item {} ``''``Verify all charts were created successfully''``''
\item {} mandatory\_\allowbreak{}charts = [
\item {} 'chart\_\allowbreak{}03\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}product\_\allowbreak{}stacked\_\allowbreak{}area.png',
\item {} 'chart\_\allowbreak{}04\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}geography\_\allowbreak{}stacked\_\allowbreak{}bar.png',

\item {} 'chart\_\allowbreak{}32\_\allowbreak{}valuation\_\allowbreak{}football\_\allowbreak{}field.png'
\item {} ]
\item {} print(``\textbackslash{}n'' + ``=''*60)
\item {} print(``CHART GENERATION VERIFICATION'')
\item {} print(``=''*60)
\item {} \# Check mandatory charts
\item {} print(``\textbackslash{}n1. MANDATORY CHARTS:'')
\item {} all\_\allowbreak{}mandatory\_\allowbreak{}present = True
\item {} for chart in mandatory\_\allowbreak{}charts:
\item {} if os.path.exists(chart):
\item {} size = os.path.getsize(chart) / 1024 \# KB
\item {} print(f`` ✓ \{chart\} (\{size:.1f\} KB)'')
\item {} else:
\item {} print(f`` ✗ MISSING: \{chart\}'')
\item {} all\_\allowbreak{}mandatory\_\allowbreak{}present = False
\item {} \# Count total charts
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if f.startswith('chart\_\allowbreak{}') and f.endswith('.png')]
\item {} print(f``\textbackslash{}n2. TOTAL CHARTS: \{len(chart\_\allowbreak{}files)\}'')
\item {} print(f`` Target: 25-35 charts'')
\item {} print(f`` Status: \{'✓ PASS' if 25 <= len(chart\_\allowbreak{}files) <= 35 else '⚠ WARNING'\}'')
\item {} \# Check file sizes (should be > 50KB for 300 DPI)
\item {} print(``\textbackslash{}n3. FILE SIZE CHECK:'')
\item {} small\_\allowbreak{}files = []
\item {} for chart in chart\_\allowbreak{}files[:5]: \# Sample first 5
\item {} size = os.path.getsize(chart) / 1024
\item {} if size < 50:
\item {} small\_\allowbreak{}files.append(chart)
\item {} print(f`` \{chart\}: \{size:.1f\} KB'')
\item {} if small\_\allowbreak{}files:
\item {} print(f`` ⚠ WARNING: \{len(small\_\allowbreak{}files)\} files may be low resolution'')
\item {} else:
\item {} print(f`` ✓ All sampled files have adequate size'')
\item {} \# Final verdict
\item {} print(``\textbackslash{}n'' + ``=''*60)
\item {} if all\_\allowbreak{}mandatory\_\allowbreak{}present and 25 <= len(chart\_\allowbreak{}files) <= 35:
\item {} print(``✓ VERIFICATION PASSED - Ready for Task 5'')
\item {} else:
\item {} print(``✗ VERIFICATION FAILED - Review missing charts'')
\item {} print(``=''*60 + ``\textbackslash{}n'')
\item {} verify\_\allowbreak{}charts()
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Quality Standards}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Visual Quality}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox High resolution (300 DPI minimum)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Professional color scheme (consistent across all charts)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Clear, legible text (no fonts smaller than 9pt)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Proper aspect ratio (no distortion)
\item {} \UncheckedBox No pixelation or artifacts
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Data Accuracy}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Data matches source (financial model and valuation)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Proper units and labels (\$ millions, percentages, etc.)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Appropriate scale and range
\item {} \UncheckedBox Consistent time periods across charts
\item {} \UncheckedBox Verified calculations
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Formatting Quality}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox Consistent styling across all charts
\item {} \UncheckedBox Proper figure numbering (sequential)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Clear titles and captions
\item {} \UncheckedBox Source citations on every chart
\item {} \UncheckedBox Professional appearance
\end{itemize}

\subparagraph{Completeness}\mbox{}\par
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} \UncheckedBox All 4 mandatory charts created
\item {} \UncheckedBox 25-35 total charts
\item {} \UncheckedBox Proper file naming (chart\_\allowbreak{}01, chart\_\allowbreak{}02, etc.)
\item {} \UncheckedBox Chart index created
\item {} \UncheckedBox Ready for embedding in Word
\end{itemize}

\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Chart Types Reference}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{When to Use Each Chart Type}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Line Charts}: Time series trends (revenue, margins, stock price)


\textbf{Stacked Area}: Revenue by product ⭐, market size composition


\textbf{Stacked Bar}: Revenue by geography ⭐, quarterly breakdowns


\textbf{Heatmap}: DCF sensitivity ⭐, correlation matrices


\textbf{Horizontal Bar}: Valuation football field ⭐, peer rankings


\textbf{Waterfall}: Revenue bridges, margin analysis, DCF build-up


\textbf{Scatter/Bubble}: Growth vs. valuation, competitive positioning


\textbf{2×2 Matrix}: Competitive positioning, product portfolio


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{File Naming Convention}\mbox{}\par

\textbf{Always use this format:}

\textbf{Structured Template}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}[NUMBER]\_\allowbreak{}[DESCRIPTION].png
\item {} Examples:
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}01\_\allowbreak{}stock\_\allowbreak{}price\_\allowbreak{}performance.png
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}03\_\allowbreak{}revenue\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}product\_\allowbreak{}stacked\_\allowbreak{}area.png
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}28\_\allowbreak{}dcf\_\allowbreak{}sensitivity\_\allowbreak{}heatmap.png
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Number charts sequentially} based on their position in the report, not creation order.


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

\subparagraph{Common Chart Generation Issues}\mbox{}\par

\subparagraph{Issue 1: Low Resolution}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Problem}: Chart looks pixelated \textbf{Solution}: Ensure \emph{dpi=300} in \emph{plt.savefig()}


\subparagraph{Issue 2: Text Cutoff}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Problem}: Labels or titles cut off at edges \textbf{Solution}: Use \emph{bbox\_\allowbreak{}inches='tight'} in \emph{plt.savefig()}


\subparagraph{Issue 3: Poor Colors}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Problem}: Colors don't look professional \textbf{Solution}: Use established palettes like Tableau10 or define custom corporate colors


\subparagraph{Issue 4: Overlapping Labels}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Problem}: Axis labels overlap \textbf{Solution}: Rotate labels (e.g., \emph{rotation=45}) or reduce font size


\subparagraph{Issue 5: White Space}\mbox{}\par
\textbf{Problem}: Too much white space around chart \textbf{Solution}: Use \emph{plt.tight\_\allowbreak{}layout()} before saving


\vspace{0.5em}\hrule\vspace{0.5em}

A successful chart package should:

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=2pt,topsep=2pt]
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}03: Revenue by Product
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}04: Revenue by Geography
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}28: DCF Sensitivity
\item {} chart\_\allowbreak{}32: Valuation Football Field
\end{itemize}
\item {} \textbf{Create 25 required charts minimum} (verified)
\item {} \textbf{Optional: 1-10 additional charts} for 26-35 total
\item {} Have consistent professional styling across all charts
\item {} Be high resolution (300 DPI) for print quality
\item {} Have clear labels, legends, and titles on every chart
\item {} Include proper figure numbers and source citations
\item {} Be ready for immediate embedding in Word
\item {} Cover all key financial metrics and analyses
\item {} Tell a visual story complementing the written analysis
\item {} Be accurate and auditable to source data (model/valuation)
\item {} All charts packaged in zip file with chart index
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Remember}: Task 5 will embed ALL charts created (25-35) throughout the report for visual density.
